 * order. __builtin_floor maps to wasm f64.floor (exact).
 *
 * Sixth reconstructed primitive of the approach-B port; third asset decoder.
 */
#include <stdint.h>

#ifdef __EMSCRIPTEN__
#include <emscripten.h>
#define EXPORT EMSCRIPTEN_KEEPALIVE
#else
#define EXPORT
#endif

static inline uint16_t ru16(const uint8_t *p) { uint16_t v; __builtin_memcpy(&v, p, 2); return v; }
static inline uint32_t ru32(const uint8_t *p) { uint32_t v; __builtin_memcpy(&v, p, 4); return v; }
static inline int32_t  ri32(const uint8_t *p) { int32_t v;  __builtin_memcpy(&v, p, 4); return v; }

/* IDCT basis M[x*8+u] = C[u]*cos((2x+1)u*pi/16), exact bits (see scratchpad/gen_m.js). */
static const uint64_t M_BITS[64] = {
    0x3fd6a09e667f3bcdULL, 0x3fdf6297cff75cb0ULL, 0x3fdd906bcf328d46ULL, 0x3fda9b66290ea1a3ULL,
    0x3fd6a09e667f3bcdULL, 0x3fd1c73b39ae68c9ULL, 0x3fc87de2a6aea964ULL, 0x3fb8f8b83c69a60dULL,
    0x3fd6a09e667f3bcdULL, 0x3fda9b66290ea1a3ULL, 0x3fc87de2a6aea964ULL, 0xbfb8f8b83c69a608ULL,
    0xbfd6a09e667f3bccULL, 0xbfdf6297cff75cb0ULL, 0xbfdd906bcf328d47ULL, 0xbfd1c73b39ae68c8ULL,
    0x3fd6a09e667f3bcdULL, 0x3fd1c73b39ae68c9ULL, 0xbfc87de2a6aea962ULL, 0xbfdf6297cff75cb0ULL,
    0xbfd6a09e667f3bceULL, 0x3fb8f8b83c69a60cULL, 0x3fdd906bcf328d44ULL, 0x3fda9b66290ea1a6ULL,
    0x3fd6a09e667f3bcdULL, 0x3fb8f8b83c69a60dULL, 0xbfdd906bcf328d46ULL, 0xbfd1c73b39ae68c8ULL,
    0x3fd6a09e667f3bcbULL, 0x3fda9b66290ea1a6ULL, 0xbfc87de2a6aea965ULL, 0xbfdf6297cff75cb2ULL,
    0x3fd6a09e667f3bcdULL, 0xbfb8f8b83c69a608ULL, 0xbfdd906bcf328d47ULL, 0x3fd1c73b39ae68c5ULL,
    0x3fd6a09e667f3bceULL, 0xbfda9b66290ea1a2ULL, 0xbfc87de2a6aea971ULL, 0x3fdf6297cff75cb0ULL,
    0x3fd6a09e667f3bcdULL, 0xbfd1c73b39ae68c6ULL, 0xbfc87de2a6aea96dULL, 0x3fdf6297cff75cb0ULL,
    0xbfd6a09e667f3bc5ULL, 0xbfb8f8b83c69a602ULL, 0x3fdd906bcf328d46ULL, 0xbfda9b66290ea1a1ULL,
    0x3fd6a09e667f3bcdULL, 0xbfda9b66290ea1a4ULL, 0x3fc87de2a6aea967ULL, 0x3fb8f8b83c69a61dULL,
    0xbfd6a09e667f3bc9ULL, 0x3fdf6297cff75cb2ULL, 0xbfdd906bcf328d43ULL, 0x3fd1c73b39ae68c2ULL,
    0x3fd6a09e667f3bcdULL, 0xbfdf6297cff75cb0ULL, 0x3fdd906bcf328d44ULL, 0xbfda9b66290ea1a2ULL,
    0x3fd6a09e667f3bc4ULL, 0xbfd1c73b39ae68c2ULL, 0x3fc87de2a6aea95fULL, 0xbfb8f8b83c69a616ULL,
};

static const int ZIGZAG[64] = {0,1,8,16,9,2,3,10,17,24,32,25,18,11,4,5,12,19,26,33,40,48,41,34,27,20,
    13,6,7,14,21,28,35,42,49,56,57,50,43,36,29,22,15,23,30,37,44,51,58,59,52,45,38,31,
    39,46,53,60,61,54,47,55,62,63};
static const int QLUMA[64] = {16,11,10,16,24,40,51,61,12,12,14,19,26,58,60,55,14,13,16,24,40,57,69,56,
    14,17,22,29,51,87,80,62,18,22,37,56,68,109,103,77,24,35,55,64,81,104,113,92,
    49,64,78,87,103,121,120,101,72,92,95,98,112,100,103,99};
static const int QCHROMA[64] = {17,18,24,47,99,99,99,99,18,21,26,66,99,99,99,99,24,26,56,99,99,99,99,99,
    47,66,99,99,99,99,99,99,99,99,99,99,99,99,99,99,99,99,99,99,99,99,99,99,
    99,99,99,99,99,99,99,99,99,99,99,99,99,99,99,99};

/* AXIS.EXE Huffman table VAs (SPEC §2.2): 2 DC (12 entries, stride 0x60),
 * 2 AC (256 entries, stride 0x800). Each entry is (int32 len, int32 code). */
#define DC_VA 0x4c6a98u
#define DC_STRIDE 0x60u
#define AC_VA 0x4c6b58u
#define AC_STRIDE 0x800u

#define MAXWH (1024 * 1024)
static uint8_t PLANE[4][MAXWH];

static uint32_t va_to_off(const uint8_t *exe, uint32_t exeLen, uint32_t va) {
    uint32_t eLfanew = ru32(exe + 0x3c);
    uint16_t nsec = ru16(exe + eLfanew + 6);
    uint16_t opt  = ru16(exe + eLfanew + 20);
    uint32_t base = ru32(exe + eLfanew + 24 + 28);
    uint32_t rva = va - base, sec = eLfanew + 24 + opt;
    for (uint16_t i = 0; i < nsec; i++) {
        uint32_t s = sec + (uint32_t)i * 40;
        uint32_t vs = ru32(exe + s + 8), vaddr = ru32(exe + s + 12), praw = ru32(exe + s + 20);
        uint32_t span = (vs > 1 ? vs : 1) + 0x2000;
        if (rva >= vaddr && rva < vaddr + span) return praw + (rva - vaddr);
    }
    (void)exeLen;
    return 0xffffffffu;
}

/* ext(v,n): sign-extend an n-bit magnitude, JPEG style. */
static inline int ext_(uint32_t v, int n) { return ((int)v >= (1 << (n - 1))) ? (int)v : (int)v - (1 << n) + 1; }

/* round-half-to-even, matching Python round() / web/ptx.js rhe(). */
static inline double rhe_(double x) {
    double f = __builtin_floor(x), d = x - f;
    if (d < 0.5) return f;
    if (d > 0.5) return f + 1.0;
    long long fi = (long long)f;
    return (fi % 2 == 0) ? f : f + 1.0;
}

EXPORT
int axis_ptx_decode(const uint8_t *ptx, uint32_t ptxLen, const uint8_t *exe, uint32_t exeLen,
                    uint8_t *rgbaOut, uint32_t *wh) {
    if (!(ptx[0] == 0x50 && ptx[1] == 0x54 && ptx[2] == 0x58 && ptx[3] == 0)) return -1;  /* "PTX\0" */
    uint32_t w8 = ru16(ptx + 6), h8 = ru16(ptx + 8);
    uint32_t fmt = ru16(ptx + 10), quality = ru16(ptx + 12);
    uint32_t w = w8 * 8, h = h8 * 8;
    int planes = (fmt == 0x82) ? 4 : 3;
    if (w * h > (uint32_t)MAXWH) return -2;

    double M[64];
    for (int i = 0; i < 64; i++) __builtin_memcpy(&M[i], &M_BITS[i], 8);

    /* Huffman tables from the EXE. */
    int dcLen[2][12], dcCode[2][12], acLen[2][256], acCode[2][256];
    for (int t = 0; t < 2; t++) {
        uint32_t off = va_to_off(exe, exeLen, DC_VA + (uint32_t)t * DC_STRIDE);
        if (off == 0xffffffffu) return -3;
        for (int i = 0; i < 12; i++) { dcLen[t][i] = ri32(exe + off + i * 8); dcCode[t][i] = ri32(exe + off + i * 8 + 4); }
    }
    for (int t = 0; t < 2; t++) {
        uint32_t off = va_to_off(exe, exeLen, AC_VA + (uint32_t)t * AC_STRIDE);
        if (off == 0xffffffffu) return -3;
        for (int i = 0; i < 256; i++) { acLen[t][i] = ri32(exe + off + i * 8); acCode[t][i] = ri32(exe + off + i * 8 + 4); }
    }

    /* scaled quant tables */
    int ql[64], qc[64];
    for (int i = 0; i < 64; i++) {
        int a = (int)((double)(QLUMA[i] * 100) / (double)quality);   if (a < 1) a = 1; ql[i] = a;
        int c = (int)((double)(QCHROMA[i] * 100) / (double)quality); if (c < 1) c = 1; qc[i] = c;
    }

    /* MSB-first bit reader over the payload; bytes past the end read as 0 (padding). */
    uint32_t bp = 16 * 8;
    uint32_t totalBits = ptxLen * 8;
    #define BIT(i) (((uint32_t)(((i) >> 3) < ptxLen ? ptx[(i) >> 3] : 0) >> (7 - ((i) & 7))) & 1u)

    uint32_t bx = w >> 3, by = h >> 3;
    double tmp[64], out[64], coef[64];

    for (int p = 0; p < planes; p++) {
        int mode = (p == 1 || p == 2) ? 1 : 0;
        const int *q = mode ? qc : ql;
        const int *dcL = dcLen[mode], *dcC = dcCode[mode], *acL = acLen[mode], *acC = acCode[mode];
        int pred = 0, over = 0;
        uint8_t *px = PLANE[p];
        for (uint32_t i = 0; i < w * h; i++) px[i] = 128;

        for (uint32_t bb = 0; bb < bx * by && !over; bb++) {
            if (bp > totalBits + 16) { over = 1; break; }
            for (int i = 0; i < 64; i++) coef[i] = 0.0;

            /* DC */
            uint32_t pk = 0; for (int i = 0; i < 16; i++) pk = (pk << 1) | BIT(bp + i);
            int diff = 0, matched = 0;
            for (int c = 0; c < 12; c++) {
                int ln = dcL[c];
                if (ln && (pk >> (16 - ln)) == (uint32_t)dcC[c]) {
                    bp += ln;
                    if (c != 0) { uint32_t v = 0; for (int i = 0; i < c; i++) v = (v << 1) | BIT(bp + i); bp += c; diff = ext_(v, c); }
                    matched = 1; break;
                }
            }
            if (!matched) return -4;
            pred += diff; coef[0] = (double)pred;

            /* AC */
            int k = 1;
            while (k < 64) {
                uint32_t apk = 0; for (int i = 0; i < 16; i++) apk = (apk << 1) | BIT(bp + i);
                int run = 0, size = 0, val = 0, m2 = 0;
                for (int s = 0; s < 256; s++) {
                    int ln = acL[s];
                    if (ln && (apk >> (16 - ln)) == (uint32_t)acC[s]) {
                        bp += ln; run = s >> 4; size = s & 15;
                        if (size != 0) { uint32_t v = 0; for (int i = 0; i < size; i++) v = (v << 1) | BIT(bp + i); bp += size; val = ext_(v, size); }
                        m2 = 1; break;
                    }
                }
                if (!m2) return -5;
                if (val == 0 && run == 0) break;
                k += run; if (k >= 64) break;
                coef[ZIGZAG[k]] = (double)val; k++;
            }

            for (int i = 0; i < 64; i++) coef[i] *= (double)q[i];

            /* separable IDCT (row pass then column pass), exact order */
            for (int v = 0; v < 8; v++) {
                int any = 0; for (int u = 0; u < 8; u++) if (coef[v * 8 + u] != 0.0) { any = 1; break; }
                if (!any) { for (int x = 0; x < 8; x++) tmp[v * 8 + x] = 0.0; continue; }
                for (int x = 0; x < 8; x++) { double s = 0.0; for (int u = 0; u < 8; u++) s += coef[v * 8 + u] * M[x * 8 + u]; tmp[v * 8 + x] = s; }
            }
            for (int x = 0; x < 8; x++) for (int y = 0; y < 8; y++) { double s = 0.0; for (int v = 0; v < 8; v++) s += tmp[v * 8 + x] * M[y * 8 + v]; out[y * 8 + x] = s; }

            uint32_t ox = (bb % bx) * 8, oy = (bb / bx) * 8;
            for (uint32_t yy = 0; yy < 8; yy++) for (uint32_t xx = 0; xx < 8; xx++) {
                int val = (int)rhe_(out[yy * 8 + xx]) + 128;
                if (val < 0) val = 0; else if (val > 255) val = 255;
                px[(oy + yy) * w + (ox + xx)] = (uint8_t)val;
            }
        }
        bp = (bp + 7) & ~7u;
    }

    const uint8_t *Y = PLANE[0], *Cb = PLANE[1], *Cr = PLANE[2], *A = (planes == 4) ? PLANE[3] : 0;
    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < w * h; i++) {
        double y = (double)Y[i], cb = (double)Cb[i] - 128.0, cr = (double)Cr[i] - 128.0;
        double r = y + 1.402 * cr, g = y - 0.344136 * cb - 0.714136 * cr, bl = y + 1.772 * cb;
        uint32_t j = i * 4;
        rgbaOut[j]     = r  < 0 ? 0 : r  > 255 ? 255 : (uint8_t)(int)r;
        rgbaOut[j + 1] = g  < 0 ? 0 : g  > 255 ? 255 : (uint8_t)(int)g;
        rgbaOut[j + 2] = bl < 0 ? 0 : bl > 255 ? 255 : (uint8_t)(int)bl;
        rgbaOut[j + 3] = A ? A[i] : 255;
    }
    wh[0] = w; wh[1] = h;
    return 0;
    #undef BIT
}

```

`port/src/axis_rng.c`:

```c
/* AXIS shared RNG pool — the backbone of the game's lockstep determinism.
 *
 * At startup AXIS loads a 4096-entry random pool from MATHFUNC.TBL (AXIS.EXE
 * FUN_0046b6f0) and every peer consumes it by one shared index as
 * `pool[idx++ & 0xfff] % N`, so spawns / AI / damage rolls match across clients
 * (SPEC §5). The pool is just a precomputed table of standard MSVC `rand()`
 * outputs seeded with `srand(1)` — VERIFIED: regenerating it with the MSVC LCG
 * reproduces all 4096 entries of the shipped MATHFUNC.TBL exactly (4096/4096).
 *
 * So the determinism RNG is fully reconstructable without the data file:
 *   seed = seed*214013 + 2531011 ;  value = (seed >> 16) & 0x7fff
 *
 * This lets the WASM client build the identical shared pool and stay in lockstep
 * with the original game's decisions. Third reconstructed primitive of the port
 * (after axis_crypt.c and axis_netdecode.c).
 */
#include <stdint.h>

#ifdef __EMSCRIPTEN__
#include <emscripten.h>
#define EXPORT EMSCRIPTEN_KEEPALIVE
#else
#define EXPORT
#endif

/* Fill `pool[0..4095]` with AXIS's shared RNG pool (MSVC rand() after srand(1)). */
EXPORT
void axis_rng_fill(uint32_t *pool) {
    uint32_t seed = 1;
    for (int i = 0; i < 4096; i++) {
        seed = seed * 214013u + 2531011u;
        pool[i] = (seed >> 16) & 0x7fff;
    }
}

/* One shared-pool draw, advancing the shared index: `pool[idx++ & 0xfff] % n`
 * (n == 0 returns the raw value). This is the exact consumption the game uses
 * everywhere its lockstep decisions are made — `pool[DAT_00a20488++ & 0xfff] % N`
 * in the per-tick sim and the packet builders (e.g. FUN_0048d080). It is the
 * operational core of the determinism: two peers starting at the same index and
 * drawing in the same order get identical spawns / rolls.
 *
 * VERIFIED against real recorded play: a type-1 packet's RNG fields are two
 * successive `axis_rng_draw(pool,&idx,100000)` values of this pool — matched
 * 247/247 on Test.ape by tools/verify_rng.js, and re-checked here in
 * port/wasm/verify_emcc.js by reproducing those packets' [44]/[50] from the WASM
 * draw at each packet's recovered index. */
EXPORT
uint32_t axis_rng_draw(const uint32_t *pool, uint32_t *idx, uint32_t n) {
    uint32_t v = pool[(*idx)++ & 0xfffu];
    return n ? (v % n) : v;
}

```

`port/src/axis_shp.c`:

```c
/* AXIS .shp mesh geometry decoder — reconstructed from the Shape.dat record
 * layout (docs/SPEC.md §2.1 archive + §2.8 mesh), ported byte-for-byte from the
 * verified web/shp.js / tools/parse_shp.py reference.
 *
 * A Shape.dat record is one mesh object: header 0x4C (groupCount @ +0x10,
 * tag 0x71110300 @ +0x30), then groupCount group headers of 0x80 bytes
 * (vertexCount @ +0x34), then per group a packed block of
 *   vc*pos(12) + vc*norm(12) + vc*attr(4) + vc*uv(8) + an index region of
 *   triangles (three u32, each < vc) interleaved with 12-byte descriptors whose
 *   3rd word is a stale saved pointer (0x04000000..0x40000000, skipped).
 * Group vertices are concatenated; triangle indices are made global (+base).
 *
 * This is the fourth reconstructed primitive of the approach-B port and the
 * first that is a real asset *decoder* (after the cipher / state codec / RNG):
 * it is pure parse+copy — no arithmetic — so the WASM output is bit-identical to
 * the JS/Python reference, which port/wasm/verify_emcc.js checks on every mesh
 * in the user's Shape.dat. Compiles into the standalone reactor via ../build.sh.
 *
 * Geometry only; the per-group texture index string (group+4, "01".."NN") is
 * metadata the existing name reader handles and is not needed for geometry.
 */
#include <stdint.h>

#ifdef __EMSCRIPTEN__
#include <emscripten.h>
#define EXPORT EMSCRIPTEN_KEEPALIVE
#else
#define EXPORT
#endif

static inline float    rf32(const uint8_t *p) { float v;    __builtin_memcpy(&v, p, 4); return v; }
static inline uint32_t ru32(const uint8_t *p) { uint32_t v; __builtin_memcpy(&v, p, 4); return v; }
static inline int32_t  ri32(const uint8_t *p) { int32_t v;  __builtin_memcpy(&v, p, 4); return v; }

#define OBJ_HDR 0x4c
#define GRP_HDR 0x80
#define TAG     0x71110300u

/* Parse one mesh record buf[off..end). Writes concatenated geometry into the
 * caller's output buffers and per-group [triStart,triEnd] into grpOut (2 u32
 * per group). counts := {vtotal, ttotal, gcount, consumed}. Returns 0, or -1 if
 * the tag is wrong (mirrors shp.js throwing). Caller sizes the output buffers;
 * the verifier uses generous fixed regions.
 */
EXPORT
int axis_shp_parse(const uint8_t *buf, uint32_t off, uint32_t end,
                   float *posOut, float *nrmOut, float *uvOut,
                   uint32_t *triOut, uint32_t *grpOut, uint32_t *counts) {
    if (ru32(buf + off + 0x30) != TAG) { counts[0] = counts[1] = counts[2] = counts[3] = 0; return -1; }
    int32_t gcount = ri32(buf + off + 0x10);

    uint32_t vtotal = 0, ttotal = 0;
    uint32_t p = off + OBJ_HDR + (uint32_t)gcount * GRP_HDR;

    for (int32_t g = 0; g < gcount; g++) {
        uint32_t gh = off + OBJ_HDR + (uint32_t)g * GRP_HDR;
        int32_t vc = ri32(buf + gh + 0x34);
        uint32_t base = vtotal;            /* global index offset for this group */
        uint32_t triStart = ttotal;

        for (int32_t i = 0; i < vc; i++) {
            const uint8_t *s = buf + p + (uint32_t)i * 12;
            posOut[(vtotal + i) * 3 + 0] = rf32(s);
            posOut[(vtotal + i) * 3 + 1] = rf32(s + 4);
            posOut[(vtotal + i) * 3 + 2] = rf32(s + 8);
        }
        p += (uint32_t)vc * 12;
        for (int32_t i = 0; i < vc; i++) {
            const uint8_t *s = buf + p + (uint32_t)i * 12;
            nrmOut[(vtotal + i) * 3 + 0] = rf32(s);
            nrmOut[(vtotal + i) * 3 + 1] = rf32(s + 4);
            nrmOut[(vtotal + i) * 3 + 2] = rf32(s + 8);
        }
        p += (uint32_t)vc * 12;
        p += (uint32_t)vc * 4;             /* attr (skipped) */
        for (int32_t i = 0; i < vc; i++) {
            const uint8_t *s = buf + p + (uint32_t)i * 8;
            uvOut[(vtotal + i) * 2 + 0] = rf32(s);
            uvOut[(vtotal + i) * 2 + 1] = rf32(s + 4);
        }
        p += (uint32_t)vc * 8;

        /* index region: triangles (3x u32, each < vc) interleaved with 12-byte
         * descriptors whose 3rd word is a stale pointer (0x04000000..0x40000000). */
        while (p + 12 <= end) {
            uint32_t a = ru32(buf + p), b = ru32(buf + p + 4), c = ru32(buf + p + 8);
            if (a < (uint32_t)vc && b < (uint32_t)vc && c < (uint32_t)vc) {
                triOut[ttotal * 3 + 0] = a + base;
                triOut[ttotal * 3 + 1] = b + base;
                triOut[ttotal * 3 + 2] = c + base;
                ttotal++;
                p += 12;
            } else if (c > 0x04000000u && c < 0x40000000u) {
                p += 12;
            } else {
                break;
            }
        }

        grpOut[g * 2 + 0] = triStart;
        grpOut[g * 2 + 1] = ttotal;        /* triEnd */
        vtotal += (uint32_t)vc;
    }

    counts[0] = vtotal;
    counts[1] = ttotal;
    counts[2] = (uint32_t)gcount;
    counts[3] = p - off;                   /* consumed */
    return 0;
}

```

`port/src/axis_skel.c`:

```c
/* AXIS Skeleton.dat bone-record decoder -- reconstructed from the rig format
 * (docs/SPEC.md §2.10), ported byte-for-byte from the verified web/skel.js /
 * tools/parse_skeleton.py.
 *
 * A Skeleton.dat entry is one rig: boneCount (i32 @ +0x10) then boneCount bone
 * records of 0x70 bytes starting at +0x24, each holding a cp949 name (+0),
 * parent index (i32 @ +0x40), a translation (f32[3] @ +0x44) and an axis-angle
 * rotation (angle f32 @ +0x50, unit axis f32[3] @ +0x54). This decodes those
 * records; it is pure parse+copy, so the WASM output is bit-identical to the
 * reference, which verify_emcc.js checks on every rig.
 *
 * Only the decode lives here. Building bind-pose world joints (web/skel.js
 * worldJoints) needs cos/sin/hypot on per-bone angles -- transcendental math
 * whose last-ULP result can't be guaranteed to match the JS reference across
 * libraries -- so that stays in the render layer, not this byte-exact decoder.
 *
 * Seventh reconstructed primitive of the approach-B port; fourth asset decoder.
 */
#include <stdint.h>

#ifdef __EMSCRIPTEN__
#include <emscripten.h>
#define EXPORT EMSCRIPTEN_KEEPALIVE
#else
#define EXPORT
#endif

static inline int32_t ri32(const uint8_t *p) { int32_t v; __builtin_memcpy(&v, p, 4); return v; }
static inline float   rf32(const uint8_t *p) { float v;   __builtin_memcpy(&v, p, 4); return v; }

#define STRIDE 0x70
#define FIRST  0x24

/* Parse rig buf[off..off+size). parentOut[b] = parent index; fOut[b*7+0..2] =
 * translation, fOut[b*7+3] = angle, fOut[b*7+4..6] = axis. counts := {boneCount
 * (header), emitted}. Returns emitted count. */
EXPORT
int axis_skel_parse(const uint8_t *buf, uint32_t off, uint32_t size,
                    int32_t *parentOut, float *fOut, uint32_t *counts) {
    int32_t boneCount = ri32(buf + off + 0x10);
    uint32_t emitted = 0;
    for (int32_t b = 0; b < boneCount; b++) {
        uint32_t bo = FIRST + (uint32_t)b * STRIDE;
        if (bo + STRIDE > size) break;                 /* mirrors the JS bounds break */
        const uint8_t *r = buf + off + bo;
        parentOut[emitted] = ri32(r + 0x40);
        fOut[emitted * 7 + 0] = rf32(r + 0x44);
        fOut[emitted * 7 + 1] = rf32(r + 0x48);
        fOut[emitted * 7 + 2] = rf32(r + 0x4c);
        fOut[emitted * 7 + 3] = rf32(r + 0x50);         /* angle */
        fOut[emitted * 7 + 4] = rf32(r + 0x54);
        fOut[emitted * 7 + 5] = rf32(r + 0x58);
        fOut[emitted * 7 + 6] = rf32(r + 0x5c);
        emitted++;
    }
    counts[0] = (uint32_t)boneCount;
    counts[1] = emitted;
    return (int)emitted;
}

```

`port/wasm/build_wasm.js`:

```js
'use strict';
/* Build + verify axis_crypt.wasm WITHOUT a C toolchain.
 *
 * Emscripten/clang are not available in every environment, so this hand-
 * assembles the exact same module that ../build.sh would produce from
 * ../src/axis_crypt.c: a WebAssembly module exporting `memory`, `axis_decrypt`
 * and `axis_encrypt(ptr,len,skip)`. It then instantiates it in Node, runs it on
 * the user's real encrypted game files, and asserts the output is byte-identical
 * to the reference cipher (web/bfd.js' rule). This is the approach-B WASM path,
 * proven end-to-end in-environment: real reconstructed AXIS logic running as WASM.
 *
 *   node build_wasm.js [<game_dir>]      # writes axis_crypt.wasm, verifies if game present
 */
const fs = require('fs'), path = require('path');

// ---- tiny WASM encoder -----------------------------------------------------
const uleb = n => { const b = []; do { let x = n & 0x7f; n >>>= 7; if (n) x |= 0x80; b.push(x); } while (n); return b; };
const sleb = n => { const b = []; let more = 1; while (more) { let x = n & 0x7f; n >>= 7; if ((n === 0 && !(x & 0x40)) || (n === -1 && (x & 0x40))) more = 0; else x |= 0x80; b.push(x); } return b; };
const str = s => [s.length, ...[...s].map(c => c.charCodeAt(0))];
const sec = (id, bytes) => [id, ...uleb(bytes.length), ...bytes];
const vec = arr => [...uleb(arr.length), ...arr.flat()];

// value types / opcodes
const I32 = 0x7f, F32 = 0x7d, VOID = 0x40;
const OP = { block:0x02, loop:0x03, end:0x0b, br:0x0c, br_if:0x0d, select:0x1b,
  get:0x20, set:0x21, i32load:0x28, f32load:0x2a, load8u:0x2d, load16s:0x2e,
  i32store:0x36, store8:0x3a, store16:0x3b, f32store:0x38,
  i32:0x41, f32c:0x43, add:0x6a, mul:0x6c, and:0x71, or:0x72, xor:0x73, shl:0x74, shru:0x76, geu:0x4f,
  fadd:0x92, fsub:0x93, fmul:0x94, fdiv:0x95, flt:0x5d, fgt:0x5e, conv:0xb2, trunc:0xa8 };
const f32c = x => [OP.f32c, ...new Uint8Array(Float32Array.of(x).buffer)];

// params: ptr=0 len=1 skip=2 ; locals: i=3 x=4
const P_PTR = 0, P_LEN = 1, P_SKIP = 2, L_I = 3, L_X = 4;
const addr = () => [OP.get, P_PTR, OP.get, L_I, OP.add];      // ptr + i

function bodyDecrypt() {
  const ins = [
    OP.get, P_SKIP, OP.set, L_I,
    OP.block, VOID,
      OP.loop, VOID,
        OP.get, L_I, OP.get, P_LEN, OP.geu, OP.br_if, 1,
        ...addr(), OP.load8u, 0, 0, OP.i32, ...sleb(0x59), OP.xor, OP.set, L_X,
        ...addr(),
          OP.get, L_X, OP.i32, 1, OP.shl,
          OP.get, L_X, OP.i32, 7, OP.shru,
          OP.or,
        OP.store8, 0, 0,
        OP.get, L_I, OP.i32, 1, OP.add, OP.set, L_I,
        OP.br, 0,
      OP.end,
    OP.end,
    OP.end,
  ];
  return [...vec([[2, I32]]), ...ins];   // locals: 2 x i32
}
function bodyEncrypt() {
  const ins = [
    OP.get, P_SKIP, OP.set, L_I,
    OP.block, VOID,
      OP.loop, VOID,
        OP.get, L_I, OP.get, P_LEN, OP.geu, OP.br_if, 1,
        ...addr(), OP.load8u, 0, 0, OP.set, L_X,
        ...addr(),
          OP.get, L_X, OP.i32, 1, OP.shru,
          OP.get, L_X, OP.i32, 7, OP.shl,
          OP.or,
          OP.i32, ...sleb(0x59), OP.xor,
        OP.store8, 0, 0,
        OP.get, L_I, OP.i32, 1, OP.add, OP.set, L_I,
        OP.br, 0,
      OP.end,
    OP.end,
    OP.end,
  ];
  return [...vec([[2, I32]]), ...ins];
}

// axis_decode_state(p=0, out=1) ; local raw=2 (f32). Mirrors axis_netdecode.c.
function bodyDecodeState() {
  const P = 0, O = 1, RAW = 2;
  const ld16 = off => [OP.get, P, OP.load16s, 1, ...uleb(off), OP.conv, ...f32c(10), OP.fmul];
  const ins = [
    // actor: out[0] = p[4] >> 4
    OP.get, O, OP.get, P, OP.load8u, 0, 4, OP.i32, 4, OP.shru, OP.i32store, 2, 0,
    // x/y/z at out+4/+8/+12 = int16(p+5/7/9) * 10
    OP.get, O, ...ld16(5), OP.f32store, 2, 4,
    OP.get, O, ...ld16(7), OP.f32store, 2, 8,
    OP.get, O, ...ld16(9), OP.f32store, 2, 12,
    // raw = p[0xc] * 14.0625
    OP.get, P, OP.load8u, 0, 0x0c, OP.conv, ...f32c(14.0625), OP.fmul, OP.set, RAW,
    // yaw at out+16 = select(raw-3600, raw, raw>1800) * 0.1
    OP.get, O,
      OP.get, RAW, ...f32c(3600), OP.fsub,
      OP.get, RAW,
      OP.get, RAW, ...f32c(1800), OP.fgt,
      OP.select,
      ...f32c(0.1), OP.fmul,
    OP.f32store, 2, 16,
    OP.end,
  ];
  return [...vec([[1, F32]]), ...ins];
}

// axis_encode_state(in=0, p=1) ; local raw=2 (f32). Inverse of decode; mirrors C.
function bodyEncodeState() {
  const IN = 0, P = 1, RAW = 2;
  // store16 at p+off = trunc(f32load(in+inoff) / 10)
  const q = (inoff, poff) => [OP.get, P, OP.get, IN, OP.f32load, 2, inoff, ...f32c(10), OP.fdiv, OP.trunc, OP.store16, 1, poff];
  const ins = [
    // p[1] = 0  (type)
    OP.get, P, OP.i32, 0, OP.store8, 0, 1,
    // p[4] = (actor & 0xf) << 4
    OP.get, P, OP.get, IN, OP.i32load, 2, 0, OP.i32, 0xf, OP.and, OP.i32, 4, OP.shl, OP.store8, 0, 4,
    // x/y/z -> int16 at p+5/7/9
    ...q(4, 5), ...q(8, 7), ...q(12, 9),
    // raw = yaw * 10
    OP.get, IN, OP.f32load, 2, 16, ...f32c(10), OP.fmul, OP.set, RAW,
    // if raw<0 raw += 3600   (select(raw+3600, raw, raw<0))
    OP.get, RAW, ...f32c(3600), OP.fadd,
    OP.get, RAW,
    OP.get, RAW, ...f32c(0), OP.flt,
    OP.select, OP.set, RAW,
    // p[0xc] = trunc(raw / 14.0625)
    OP.get, P, OP.get, RAW, ...f32c(14.0625), OP.fdiv, OP.trunc, OP.store8, 0, 0x0c,
    OP.end,
  ];
  return [...vec([[1, F32]]), ...ins];
}

// axis_rng_fill(out=0) ; locals i=1, seed=2. Mirrors axis_rng.c (MSVC rand, srand(1)).
function bodyRngFill() {
  const O = 0, I = 1, SEED = 2;
  const ins = [
    OP.i32, 1, OP.set, SEED,                                   // seed = 1
    OP.block, VOID,
      OP.loop, VOID,
        OP.get, I, OP.i32, ...uleb(4096), OP.geu, OP.br_if, 1,
        // seed = seed*214013 + 2531011
        OP.get, SEED, OP.i32, ...sleb(214013), OP.mul, OP.i32, ...sleb(2531011), OP.add, OP.set, SEED,
        // out[i*4] = (seed>>16) & 0x7fff
        OP.get, O, OP.get, I, OP.i32, 4, OP.mul, OP.add,
        OP.get, SEED, OP.i32, 16, OP.shru, OP.i32, ...sleb(0x7fff), OP.and,
        OP.i32store, 2, 0,
        OP.get, I, OP.i32, 1, OP.add, OP.set, I,
        OP.br, 0,
      OP.end,
    OP.end,
    OP.end,
  ];
  return [...vec([[2, I32]]), ...ins];   // locals: 2 x i32
}

function buildModule() {
  const magic = [0x00, 0x61, 0x73, 0x6d], version = [0x01, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00];
  // types: 0 = (i32,i32,i32)->() ; 1 = (i32,i32)->() ; 2 = (i32)->()
  const typeSec = sec(1, vec([
    [0x60, ...vec([I32, I32, I32]), ...vec([])],
    [0x60, ...vec([I32, I32]), ...vec([])],
    [0x60, ...vec([I32]), ...vec([])],
  ]));
  // funcs: decrypt(t0), encrypt(t0), decode_state(t1), encode_state(t1), rng_fill(t2)
  const funcSec = sec(3, vec([0, 0, 1, 1, 2]));
  // memory: one, min 256 pages (16 MiB) — fits any AXIS file in one buffer
  const memSec = sec(5, vec([[0x00, ...uleb(256)]]));
  const expSec = sec(7, vec([
    [...str('memory'), 0x02, 0],
    [...str('axis_decrypt'), 0x00, 0],
    [...str('axis_encrypt'), 0x00, 1],
    [...str('axis_decode_state'), 0x00, 2],
    [...str('axis_encode_state'), 0x00, 3],
    [...str('axis_rng_fill'), 0x00, 4],
  ]));
  const codeSec = sec(10, vec([
    [...uleb(bodyDecrypt().length), ...bodyDecrypt()],
    [...uleb(bodyEncrypt().length), ...bodyEncrypt()],
    [...uleb(bodyDecodeState().length), ...bodyDecodeState()],
    [...uleb(bodyEncodeState().length), ...bodyEncodeState()],
    [...uleb(bodyRngFill().length), ...bodyRngFill()],
  ]));
  return Uint8Array.from([...magic, ...version, ...typeSec, ...funcSec, ...memSec, ...expSec, ...codeSec]);
}

// reference cipher (same rule as web/bfd.js / tools/bfd_crypt.py)
const refDec = (b, skip) => { const o = Uint8Array.from(b); for (let i = skip; i < o.length; i++) { const x = (o[i] ^ 0x59) & 0xff; o[i] = ((x << 1) | (x >> 7)) & 0xff; } return o; };

async function main() {
  const wasm = buildModule();
  const outPath = path.join(__dirname, 'axis_crypt.wasm');
  fs.writeFileSync(outPath, wasm);
  console.log(`built ${outPath} (${wasm.length} bytes)`);

  // emit a browser loader with the module bytes embedded (so the client needs
  // no separate .wasm fetch and nothing binary is committed — crypt.js is source)
  const b64 = Buffer.from(wasm).toString('base64');
  const cryptJs = `/* AXIS primitives as WebAssembly, for the browser client.
 * Generated by port/wasm/build_wasm.js from port/src/axis_crypt.c + axis_netdecode.c
 * (docs/SPEC.md §2.3 / §4). The ${wasm.length}-byte module is embedded below.
 * AXISCrypt.load() returns a WASM-backed API (falls back to JS on failure):
 *   decrypt(u8,skip) / encrypt(u8,skip)
 *   decodeState(u8[64]) -> {actor,x,y,z,yaw}   (type-0 state packet)
 *   encodeState({actor,x,y,z,yaw}) -> u8[64]    (AXIS wire format)
 */
(function (root) {
  'use strict';
  const B64 = "${b64}";
  function bytes() { const s = atob(B64), a = new Uint8Array(s.length); for (let i = 0; i < s.length; i++) a[i] = s.charCodeAt(i); return a; }
  function jsDecrypt(u8, skip) { skip = skip || 0; for (let i = skip; i < u8.length; i++) { const x = (u8[i] ^ 0x59) & 0xff; u8[i] = ((x << 1) | (x >> 7)) & 0xff; } return u8; }
  function jsEncrypt(u8, skip) { skip = skip || 0; for (let i = skip; i < u8.length; i++) { let x = u8[i] & 0xff; x = ((x >> 1) | (x << 7)) & 0xff; u8[i] = (x ^ 0x59) & 0xff; } return u8; }
  const fr = Math.fround;
  function jsDecodeState(p) { const dv = new DataView(p.buffer, p.byteOffset, p.byteLength);
    let raw = fr(p[0x0c] * fr(14.0625)); if (raw > 1800) raw = fr(raw - 3600);
    return { actor: p[4] >> 4, x: fr(dv.getInt16(5, true) * 10), y: fr(dv.getInt16(7, true) * 10), z: fr(dv.getInt16(9, true) * 10), yaw: fr(raw * fr(0.1)) }; }
  function jsEncodeState(s) { const o = new Uint8Array(64), dv = new DataView(o.buffer);
    o[1] = 0; o[4] = ((s.actor & 0xf) << 4) & 0xff;
    dv.setInt16(5, Math.trunc(fr(fr(s.x) / 10)) | 0, true); dv.setInt16(7, Math.trunc(fr(fr(s.y) / 10)) | 0, true); dv.setInt16(9, Math.trunc(fr(fr(s.z) / 10)) | 0, true);
    let raw = fr(fr(s.yaw || 0) * 10); if (raw < 0) raw = fr(raw + 3600); o[0x0c] = Math.trunc(fr(raw / fr(14.0625))) & 0xff; return o; }
  // shared lockstep RNG pool (MSVC rand, srand(1)); SPEC §5. Same values as MATHFUNC.TBL.
  function jsRngPool() { let s = 1 >>> 0; const a = new Uint32Array(4096); for (let i = 0; i < 4096; i++) { s = (Math.imul(s, 214013) + 2531011) >>> 0; a[i] = (s >>> 16) & 0x7fff; } return a; }
  // the game's draw operation over the shared pool: pool[idx++ & 0xfff] % N (SPEC §5).
  // Two peers that start at the same index and draw in the same order get the same values.
  function makeRng(pool, start) { let idx = (start | 0) >>> 0;
    return { draw(n) { const v = pool[idx++ & 0xfff]; return n ? (v % n) >>> 0 : v; }, get index() { return idx & 0xfff; }, set index(i) { idx = (i | 0) >>> 0; } }; }
  let mod = null;
  async function load() {
    if (mod) return mod;
    try {
      const { instance } = await WebAssembly.instantiate(bytes(), {});
      const ex = instance.exports, mem = ex.memory;
      const run = fn => (u8, skip) => {
        const need = (u8.length + 0xffff) >> 16, have = mem.buffer.byteLength >> 16;
        if (need > have) mem.grow(need - have);
        const m = new Uint8Array(mem.buffer); m.set(u8, 0); fn(0, u8.length, skip || 0);
        u8.set(m.subarray(0, u8.length)); return u8;
      };
      const SB = 1 << 22, KB = SB + 64;   // struct + packet scratch (within the 16MiB min)
      const decodeState = p => { const m = new Uint8Array(mem.buffer); m.set(p.subarray(0, 64), KB); ex.axis_decode_state(KB, SB);
        const dv = new DataView(mem.buffer); return { actor: dv.getInt32(SB, true), x: dv.getFloat32(SB + 4, true), y: dv.getFloat32(SB + 8, true), z: dv.getFloat32(SB + 12, true), yaw: dv.getFloat32(SB + 16, true) }; };
      const encodeState = s => { const dv = new DataView(mem.buffer);
        dv.setInt32(SB, s.actor | 0, true); dv.setFloat32(SB + 4, s.x, true); dv.setFloat32(SB + 8, s.y, true); dv.setFloat32(SB + 12, s.z, true); dv.setFloat32(SB + 16, s.yaw || 0, true);
        new Uint8Array(mem.buffer, KB, 64).fill(0); ex.axis_encode_state(SB, KB); return new Uint8Array(mem.buffer).slice(KB, KB + 64); };
      const RB = 1 << 21;
      const rngPool = () => { ex.axis_rng_fill(RB); return new Uint32Array(mem.buffer.slice(RB, RB + 4096 * 4)); };
      mod = { decrypt: run(ex.axis_decrypt), encrypt: run(ex.axis_encrypt), decodeState, encodeState, rngPool, rng: start => makeRng(rngPool(), start || 0), backend: 'wasm' };
    } catch (e) {
      mod = { decrypt: jsDecrypt, encrypt: jsEncrypt, decodeState: jsDecodeState, encodeState: jsEncodeState, rngPool: jsRngPool, rng: start => makeRng(jsRngPool(), start || 0), backend: 'js' };
    }
    return mod;
  }
  root.AXISCrypt = { load, jsDecrypt, jsEncrypt, jsDecodeState, jsEncodeState, jsRngPool, makeRng };
  if (typeof module !== 'undefined' && module.exports) module.exports = { load, jsDecrypt, jsEncrypt, jsDecodeState, jsEncodeState, jsRngPool, makeRng };
})(typeof window !== 'undefined' ? window : globalThis);
`;
  const cryptPath = path.join(__dirname, '..', '..', 'web', 'crypt.js');
  fs.writeFileSync(cryptPath, cryptJs);
  console.log(`emitted ${cryptPath} (browser loader, ${wasm.length}-byte module embedded)`);

  const { instance } = await WebAssembly.instantiate(wasm, {});
  const { memory, axis_decrypt, axis_encrypt } = instance.exports;
  console.log('instantiated; exports:', Object.keys(instance.exports).join(', '));

  // self-test: round-trip on a fixed vector
  const mem = new Uint8Array(memory.buffer);
  const sample = Uint8Array.from([0x00, 0x01, 0x02, 0x59, 0xaa, 0xff, 0x80, 0x7f]);
  mem.set(sample, 0);
  axis_decrypt(0, sample.length, 0);
  const dec = mem.slice(0, sample.length);
  const ref = refDec(sample, 0);
  const decOK = Buffer.compare(Buffer.from(dec), Buffer.from(ref)) === 0;
  axis_encrypt(0, sample.length, 0);                 // invert in place
  const rt = mem.slice(0, sample.length);
  const rtOK = Buffer.compare(Buffer.from(rt), Buffer.from(sample)) === 0;
  console.log(`self-test: decrypt==reference ${decOK ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL'}; encrypt∘decrypt==identity ${rtOK ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL'}`);
  if (!decOK || !rtOK) process.exit(1);

  // verify on the user's real game files, if available
  const game = process.argv[2];
  if (!game) { console.log('no game dir given — skipping real-data verification'); return; }
  const targets = [];
  const mapDir = path.join(game, 'Map');
  if (fs.existsSync(mapDir)) for (const f of fs.readdirSync(mapDir).filter(f => /\.bfd$/i.test(f)).slice(0, 5)) targets.push([path.join(mapDir, f), 4]);
  const apDir = path.join(game, 'AutoPlay');
  if (fs.existsSync(apDir)) for (const f of fs.readdirSync(apDir).filter(f => /\.ape$/i.test(f))) targets.push([path.join(apDir, f), 0]);
  const sdat = path.join(game, 'Data', 'Sound.dat');
  if (fs.existsSync(sdat)) targets.push([sdat, 0]);

  let pass = 0, fail = 0;
  for (const [file, skip] of targets) {
    const raw = fs.readFileSync(file);
    if (raw.length > 256 * 65536) { console.log(`  skip ${path.basename(file)} (>16MiB)`); continue; }
    const m = new Uint8Array(memory.buffer);
    m.set(raw, 0);
    axis_decrypt(0, raw.length, skip);
    const got = Buffer.from(m.slice(0, raw.length));
    const exp = Buffer.from(refDec(raw, skip));
    const ok = Buffer.compare(got, exp) === 0;
    console.log(`  ${ok ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL'}  ${path.basename(file).padEnd(22)} ${raw.length} bytes (skip ${skip})`);
    ok ? pass++ : fail++;
  }
  console.log(`\ncipher real-data verification: ${pass} pass, ${fail} fail`);
  if (fail) process.exit(1);

  // ---- axis_decode_state: verify the WASM state decoder on real type-0 packets
  const fround = Math.fround;
  const refState = pay => {
    const dv = new DataView(pay.buffer, pay.byteOffset, pay.byteLength);
    const actor = pay[4] >> 4;
    const x = fround(dv.getInt16(5, true) * 10), y = fround(dv.getInt16(7, true) * 10), z = fround(dv.getInt16(9, true) * 10);
    let raw = fround(pay[0x0c] * fround(14.0625)); if (raw > 1800) raw = fround(raw - 3600);
    return { actor, x, y, z, yaw: fround(raw * fround(0.1)) };
  };
  const apeFile = game && path.join(game, 'AutoPlay', 'Autoplay001.ape');
  if (apeFile && fs.existsSync(apeFile)) {
    const raw = fs.readFileSync(apeFile);
    const m = new Uint8Array(memory.buffer);
    m.set(raw, 0);
    axis_decrypt(0, raw.length, 0);                 // WASM decrypt the whole replay
    const dec = m.slice(0, raw.length);
    let start = -1;
    for (let o = 0; o < 4096 && start < 0; o++) {
      let ok = true;
      for (let k = 0; k < 3; k++) { const b = o + k * 76; if (b + 76 > dec.length || new DataView(dec.buffer, b + 8, 4).getUint32(0, true) !== 0x40) { ok = false; break; } }
      if (ok) start = o;
    }
    const fr = Math.fround;
    const refEncode = s => {
      const o = new Uint8Array(64), dv = new DataView(o.buffer);
      o[1] = 0; o[4] = ((s.actor & 0xf) << 4) & 0xff;
      dv.setInt16(5, Math.trunc(fr(fr(s.x) / 10)) | 0, true);
      dv.setInt16(7, Math.trunc(fr(fr(s.y) / 10)) | 0, true);
      dv.setInt16(9, Math.trunc(fr(fr(s.z) / 10)) | 0, true);
      let raw = fr(fr(s.yaw) * 10); if (raw < 0) raw = fr(raw + 3600);
      o[0x0c] = Math.trunc(fr(raw / fr(14.0625))) & 0xff;
      return o;
    };
    const PB = 1 << 20, OB = PB + 64, EB = PB + 256;
    let sp = 0, sf = 0, ep = 0, ef = 0, rt = 0, shown = 0;
    const FIELDS = [1, 4, 5, 6, 7, 8, 9, 10, 0x0c];
    for (let o = start; o + 76 <= dec.length; o += 76) {
      if (new DataView(dec.buffer, o + 8, 4).getUint32(0, true) !== 0x40) break;
      const pay = dec.slice(o + 12, o + 12 + 64);
      if (pay[1] !== 0) continue;
      let mm = new Uint8Array(memory.buffer); mm.set(pay, PB);
      instance.exports.axis_decode_state(PB, OB);
      const dv = new DataView(memory.buffer);
      const got = { actor: dv.getInt32(OB, true), x: dv.getFloat32(OB + 4, true), y: dv.getFloat32(OB + 8, true), z: dv.getFloat32(OB + 12, true), yaw: dv.getFloat32(OB + 16, true) };
      const exp = refState(pay);
      const okD = got.actor === exp.actor && got.x === exp.x && got.y === exp.y && got.z === exp.z && got.yaw === exp.yaw;
      okD ? sp++ : sf++;
      // encode the decoded struct back to a packet
      instance.exports.axis_encode_state(OB, EB);
      const enc = new Uint8Array(memory.buffer).slice(EB, EB + 64);
      const ref = refEncode(got);
      const okE = FIELDS.every(i => enc[i] === ref[i]);
      okE ? ep++ : ef++;
      // round-trip: does the re-encoded packet recover the original quantised fields?
      if ([5, 6, 7, 8, 9, 10, 0x0c].every(i => enc[i] === pay[i])) rt++;
      if (shown < 3) { console.log(`  state: actor ${got.actor} pos (${got.x},${got.y},${got.z}) yaw ${got.yaw.toFixed(2)}  decode ${okD ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL'}  encode ${okE ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL'}`); shown++; }
    }
    const total = sp + sf;
    console.log(`\nstate-decode  : ${sp}/${total} pass (vs reference, f32-exact)`);
    console.log(`state-encode  : ${ep}/${total} pass (vs reference, f32-exact)`);
    console.log(`encode∘decode : ${rt}/${total} recover the original quantised packet fields`);
    if (sf || ef) process.exit(1);
  }

  // ---- axis_rng_fill: WASM-build the shared RNG pool, verify vs MATHFUNC.TBL
  const RB = 1 << 21;
  instance.exports.axis_rng_fill(RB);
  const pool = new Uint32Array(memory.buffer.slice(RB, RB + 4096 * 4));
  // reference LCG (MSVC rand, srand(1))
  let seed = 1 >>> 0; const rngRef = new Uint32Array(4096);
  for (let i = 0; i < 4096; i++) { seed = (Math.imul(seed, 214013) + 2531011) >>> 0; rngRef[i] = (seed >>> 16) & 0x7fff; }
  let rp = 0; for (let i = 0; i < 4096; i++) if (pool[i] === rngRef[i]) rp++;
  console.log(`\nrng-fill (WASM)      : ${rp}/4096 match the MSVC rand(srand(1)) reference`);
  const mt = game && path.join(game, 'MATHFUNC.TBL');
  if (mt && fs.existsSync(mt)) {
    const raw = fs.readFileSync(mt);
    const file = new Uint32Array(raw.buffer.slice(raw.byteOffset + 3600 * 12, raw.byteOffset + 3600 * 12 + 4096 * 4));
    let fp = 0; for (let i = 0; i < 4096; i++) if (pool[i] === file[i]) fp++;
    console.log(`rng-fill vs MATHFUNC.TBL: ${fp}/4096 match the shipped game pool`);
    if (fp !== 4096) process.exit(1);
  }
  if (rp !== 4096) process.exit(1);
}
main().catch(e => { console.error(e); process.exit(1); });

```

`port/wasm/verify_emcc.js`:

```js
'use strict';
/* Verify the Emscripten-built standalone WASM (port/build.sh output) against the
 * user's real game data, exactly as port/wasm/build_wasm.js verifies the hand-
 * assembled module. Run inside WSL (or any box with the emcc build + Node):
 *
 *   node verify_emcc.js <axis.wasm> <game_dir>
 *
 * Checks, all byte/f32-exact vs the independent JS reference:
 *   - axis_decrypt on real Map/*.bfd, AutoPlay/*.ape, Data/Sound.dat
 *   - axis_decode_state / axis_encode_state on real type-0 replay packets
 *   - axis_rng_fill vs the MSVC LCG reference AND the shipped MATHFUNC.TBL pool
 *
 * Unlike the hand-assembled module (which has nothing at linear-memory offset 0),
 * the Emscripten module keeps static data + the C stack near offset 0, so all
 * scratch buffers live at BASE = 16 MiB and up.
 */
const fs = require('fs'), path = require('path');
const fr = Math.fround;
const BASE = 0x1000000;                       // 16 MiB floor, clear of static+stack

const refDec = (b, skip) => { const o = Uint8Array.from(b); for (let i = skip; i < o.length; i++) { const x = (o[i] ^ 0x59) & 0xff; o[i] = ((x << 1) | (x >> 7)) & 0xff; } return o; };
const refState = pay => {
  const dv = new DataView(pay.buffer, pay.byteOffset, pay.byteLength);
  let raw = fr(pay[0x0c] * fr(14.0625)); if (raw > 1800) raw = fr(raw - 3600);
  return { actor: pay[4] >> 4, x: fr(dv.getInt16(5, true) * 10), y: fr(dv.getInt16(7, true) * 10), z: fr(dv.getInt16(9, true) * 10), yaw: fr(raw * fr(0.1)) };
};
const refEncode = s => {
  const o = new Uint8Array(64), dv = new DataView(o.buffer);
  o[1] = 0; o[4] = ((s.actor & 0xf) << 4) & 0xff;
  dv.setInt16(5, Math.trunc(fr(fr(s.x) / 10)) | 0, true);
  dv.setInt16(7, Math.trunc(fr(fr(s.y) / 10)) | 0, true);
  dv.setInt16(9, Math.trunc(fr(fr(s.z) / 10)) | 0, true);
  let raw = fr(fr(s.yaw) * 10); if (raw < 0) raw = fr(raw + 3600);
  o[0x0c] = Math.trunc(fr(raw / fr(14.0625))) & 0xff;
  return o;
};

async function main() {
  const wasmPath = process.argv[2], game = process.argv[3];
  if (!wasmPath) { console.error('usage: node verify_emcc.js <axis.wasm> [game_dir]'); process.exit(2); }
  const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

  // Standalone reactor: satisfy any emscripten/wasi imports with no-ops so it
  // instantiates headless. A pure-leaf module usually needs none, but be safe.
  const imports = { env: {}, wasi_snapshot_preview1: {} };
  const proxy = new Proxy(imports, { get: (t, k) => t[k] || (t[k] = new Proxy({}, { get: () => () => 0 })) });
  let instance;
  try { ({ instance } = await WebAssembly.instantiate(wasm, proxy)); }
  catch (e) { console.error('instantiate failed:', e.message); process.exit(1); }
  const ex = instance.exports;
  const memory = ex.memory;
  console.log('instantiated; exports:', Object.keys(ex).filter(k => /axis|memory/.test(k)).join(', '));
  // resolve exports whether or not emscripten kept the leading underscore
  const F = n => ex[n] || ex['_' + n];
  const decrypt = F('axis_decrypt'), encrypt = F('axis_encrypt'),
        decodeState = F('axis_decode_state'), encodeState = F('axis_encode_state'),
        rngFill = F('axis_rng_fill'), rngDraw = F('axis_rng_draw'), shpParse = F('axis_shp_parse'), bfdExtract = F('axis_bfd_extract'),
        ptxDecode = F('axis_ptx_decode'), skelParse = F('axis_skel_parse'), apeParse = F('axis_ape_parse');
  for (const [n, f] of [['axis_decrypt', decrypt], ['axis_encrypt', encrypt], ['axis_decode_state', decodeState], ['axis_encode_state', encodeState], ['axis_rng_fill', rngFill]])
    if (typeof f !== 'function') { console.error('missing export', n); process.exit(1); }

  const pages = () => memory.buffer.byteLength >> 16;
  const ensure = need => { const have = pages(); const want = (need + 0xffff) >> 16; if (want > have) memory.grow(want - have); };

  let fails = 0;

  // ---- self-test
  {
    const sample = Uint8Array.from([0x00, 0x01, 0x02, 0x59, 0xaa, 0xff, 0x80, 0x7f]);
    ensure(BASE + sample.length);
    new Uint8Array(memory.buffer).set(sample, BASE);
    decrypt(BASE, sample.length, 0);
    const dec = new Uint8Array(memory.buffer).slice(BASE, BASE + sample.length);
    const decOK = Buffer.compare(Buffer.from(dec), Buffer.from(refDec(sample, 0))) === 0;
    encrypt(BASE, sample.length, 0);
    const rt = new Uint8Array(memory.buffer).slice(BASE, BASE + sample.length);
    const rtOK = Buffer.compare(Buffer.from(rt), Buffer.from(sample)) === 0;
    console.log(`self-test: decrypt==reference ${decOK ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL'}; encrypt∘decrypt==identity ${rtOK ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL'}`);
    if (!decOK || !rtOK) fails++;
  }

  if (!game) { console.log('no game dir — skipping real-data checks'); process.exit(fails ? 1 : 0); }

  // ---- cipher on real files
  const targets = [];
  const mapDir = path.join(game, 'Map');
  if (fs.existsSync(mapDir)) for (const f of fs.readdirSync(mapDir).filter(f => /\.bfd$/i.test(f)).slice(0, 5)) targets.push([path.join(mapDir, f), 4]);
  const apDir = path.join(game, 'AutoPlay');
  if (fs.existsSync(apDir)) for (const f of fs.readdirSync(apDir).filter(f => /\.ape$/i.test(f))) targets.push([path.join(apDir, f), 0]);
  const sdat = path.join(game, 'Data', 'Sound.dat');
  if (fs.existsSync(sdat)) targets.push([sdat, 0]);
  let pass = 0, fail = 0;
  for (const [file, skip] of targets) {
    const raw = fs.readFileSync(file);
    ensure(BASE + raw.length);
    new Uint8Array(memory.buffer).set(raw, BASE);
    decrypt(BASE, raw.length, skip);
    const got = Buffer.from(new Uint8Array(memory.buffer).slice(BASE, BASE + raw.length));
    const ok = Buffer.compare(got, Buffer.from(refDec(raw, skip))) === 0;
    console.log(`  ${ok ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL'}  ${path.basename(file).padEnd(22)} ${raw.length} bytes (skip ${skip})`);
    ok ? pass++ : fail++;
  }
  console.log(`cipher real-data: ${pass} pass, ${fail} fail`);
  fails += fail;

  // ---- state decode/encode on real type-0 packets
  const apeFile = path.join(game, 'AutoPlay', 'Autoplay001.ape');
  if (fs.existsSync(apeFile)) {
    const raw = fs.readFileSync(apeFile);
    ensure(BASE + raw.length);
    new Uint8Array(memory.buffer).set(raw, BASE);
    decrypt(BASE, raw.length, 0);
    const dec = new Uint8Array(memory.buffer).slice(BASE, BASE + raw.length);
    let start = -1;
    for (let o = 0; o < 4096 && start < 0; o++) {
      let ok = true;
      for (let k = 0; k < 3; k++) { const b = o + k * 76; if (b + 76 > dec.length || new DataView(dec.buffer, b + 8, 4).getUint32(0, true) !== 0x40) { ok = false; break; } }
      if (ok) start = o;
    }
    const PB = BASE, OB = BASE + 64, EB = BASE + 256;
    let sp = 0, sf = 0, ep = 0, ef = 0, rt = 0, total = 0, shown = 0;
    const FIELDS = [1, 4, 5, 6, 7, 8, 9, 10, 0x0c];
    for (let o = start; o >= 0 && o + 76 <= dec.length; o += 76) {
      if (new DataView(dec.buffer, o + 8, 4).getUint32(0, true) !== 0x40) break;
      const pay = dec.slice(o + 12, o + 12 + 64);
      if (pay[1] !== 0) continue;
      new Uint8Array(memory.buffer).set(pay, PB);
      decodeState(PB, OB);
      const dv = new DataView(memory.buffer);
      const got = { actor: dv.getInt32(OB, true), x: dv.getFloat32(OB + 4, true), y: dv.getFloat32(OB + 8, true), z: dv.getFloat32(OB + 12, true), yaw: dv.getFloat32(OB + 16, true) };
      const exp = refState(pay);
      (got.actor === exp.actor && got.x === exp.x && got.y === exp.y && got.z === exp.z && got.yaw === exp.yaw) ? sp++ : sf++;
      encodeState(OB, EB);
      const enc = new Uint8Array(memory.buffer).slice(EB, EB + 64);
      const ref = refEncode(got);
      FIELDS.every(i => enc[i] === ref[i]) ? ep++ : ef++;
      if ([5, 6, 7, 8, 9, 10, 0x0c].every(i => enc[i] === pay[i])) rt++;
      total++;
      if (shown < 3) { console.log(`  state: actor ${got.actor} pos (${got.x},${got.y},${got.z}) yaw ${got.yaw.toFixed(2)}  decode ${sf ? 'FAIL' : 'PASS'}`); shown++; }
    }
    console.log(`state-decode  : ${sp}/${total} pass (f32-exact)`);
    console.log(`state-encode  : ${ep}/${total} pass (f32-exact)`);
    console.log(`encode∘decode : ${rt}/${total} recover original quantised fields`);
    fails += sf + ef;
  }

  // ---- rng pool
  {
    const RB = BASE;
    ensure(RB + 4096 * 4);
    rngFill(RB);
    const pool = new Uint32Array(memory.buffer.slice(RB, RB + 4096 * 4));
    let seed = 1 >>> 0; const rngRef = new Uint32Array(4096);
    for (let i = 0; i < 4096; i++) { seed = (Math.imul(seed, 214013) + 2531011) >>> 0; rngRef[i] = (seed >>> 16) & 0x7fff; }
    let rp = 0; for (let i = 0; i < 4096; i++) if (pool[i] === rngRef[i]) rp++;
    console.log(`rng-fill (WASM)       : ${rp}/4096 match MSVC rand(srand(1))`);
    if (rp !== 4096) fails++;
    const mt = path.join(game, 'MATHFUNC.TBL');
    if (fs.existsSync(mt)) {
      const r = fs.readFileSync(mt);
      const file = new Uint32Array(r.buffer.slice(r.byteOffset + 3600 * 12, r.byteOffset + 3600 * 12 + 4096 * 4));
      let fp = 0; for (let i = 0; i < 4096; i++) if (pool[i] === file[i]) fp++;
      console.log(`rng-fill vs MATHFUNC.TBL: ${fp}/4096 match shipped game pool`);
      if (fp !== 4096) fails++;
    }

    // ---- axis_rng_draw: the lockstep draw primitive, reproduced on a REAL match.
    // A type-1 packet's RNG fields are two successive pool draws (SPEC §4,
    // FUN_0048d080): [44]=pool[idx]%100000, [50]=pool[idx+1]%100000. Recover each
    // packet's idx from the pool and check the WASM draw reproduces [44]/[50].
    if (typeof rngDraw === 'function') {
      let AP2; try { AP2 = require(path.join(__dirname, '..', '..', 'web', 'ape.js')); } catch (e) { AP2 = null; }
      const ape = path.join(game, 'AutoPlay', 'Test.ape');
      if (AP2 && fs.existsSync(ape)) {
        const pairIdx = new Map();
        for (let i = 0; i < 4096; i++) { const k = pool[i] * 100000 + pool[(i + 1) & 0xfff]; if (!pairIdx.has(k)) pairIdx.set(k, i); }
        const raw = fs.readFileSync(ape);
        const u8 = AP2.decrypt(raw.buffer.slice(raw.byteOffset, raw.byteOffset + raw.byteLength));
        const dv = new DataView(u8.buffer, u8.byteOffset, u8.byteLength);
        let start = -1;
        for (let o = 0; o < Math.min(u8.length, 4096) && start < 0; o++) { let ok = true; for (let k = 0; k < 3; k++) { const b = o + k * 76; if (b + 76 > u8.length || dv.getUint32(b + 8, true) !== 0x40) { ok = false; break; } } if (ok) start = o; }
        const IDX = RB + 4096 * 4;                 // scratch u32 for the draw index
        const idxV = new Uint32Array(memory.buffer, IDX, 1);
        let drew = 0, okd = 0;
        for (let o = start; o >= 0 && o + 76 <= u8.length; o += 76) {
          if (dv.getUint32(o + 8, true) !== 0x40) break;
          const p = o + 12; if (u8[p + 1] !== 1) continue;
          const v44 = dv.getUint16(p + 44, true), v50 = dv.getUint16(p + 50, true);
          if ((v44 === 0 && v50 === 0) || v44 >= 100000 || v50 >= 100000) continue;
          const i0 = pairIdx.get(v44 * 100000 + v50); if (i0 === undefined) continue;
          drew++;
          idxV[0] = i0;
          const d1 = rngDraw(RB, IDX, 100000), d2 = rngDraw(RB, IDX, 100000);
          if (d1 === v44 && d2 === v50 && idxV[0] === ((i0 + 2) & 0xffffffff)) okd++;
        }
        console.log(`rng-draw (WASM) on Test.ape: ${okd}/${drew} type-1 packets' [44]/[50] reproduced by the pool draw`);
        if (drew === 0 || okd !== drew) fails++;
      }
    }
  }

  // ---- axis_shp_parse: WASM mesh decoder vs web/shp.js over every Shape.dat record
  const shapeDat = path.join(game, 'Data', 'Shape.dat');
  if (typeof shpParse === 'function' && fs.existsSync(shapeDat)) {
    let SHP;
    try { SHP = require(path.join(__dirname, '..', '..', 'web', 'shp.js')); }
    catch (e) { console.log('shp.js not loadable — skipping mesh check:', e.message); SHP = null; }
    if (SHP) {
      const raw = fs.readFileSync(shapeDat);
      const ab = raw.buffer.slice(raw.byteOffset, raw.byteOffset + raw.byteLength);
      const toc = SHP.readTOC(ab);
      // memory layout: file at FILE, output buffers above it (no grow inside loop)
      const FILE = BASE;                                   // 16 MiB
      const VCAP = 200000, TCAP = 400000, GCAP = 4096;
      const POS = 0x1C00000, NRM = POS + VCAP * 12, UV = NRM + VCAP * 12,
            TRI = UV + VCAP * 8, GRP = TRI + TCAP * 12, CNT = GRP + GCAP * 8;
      ensure(CNT + 64);
      new Uint8Array(memory.buffer).set(new Uint8Array(ab), FILE);
      const buf = memory.buffer;
      const posV = new Float32Array(buf, POS, VCAP * 3), nrmV = new Float32Array(buf, NRM, VCAP * 3),
            uvV = new Float32Array(buf, UV, VCAP * 2), triV = new Uint32Array(buf, TRI, TCAP * 3),
            grpV = new Uint32Array(buf, GRP, GCAP * 2), cntV = new Uint32Array(buf, CNT, 4);
      const eqF = (a, b, n) => { for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) if (!Object.is(a[i], b[i])) return i; return -1; };

      let ok = 0, bad = 0, rej = 0, over = 0, firstBad = null;
      for (const t of toc) {
        let js = null, threw = false;
        try { js = SHP.parseShape(ab, t.off, t.end); } catch (e) { threw = true; }
        const ret = shpParse(FILE, t.off, t.end, POS, NRM, UV, TRI, GRP, CNT);
        if (threw) { if (ret === -1) rej++; else { bad++; firstBad = firstBad || `${t.name}: js threw but wasm ret ${ret}`; } continue; }
        if (js.pos.length > VCAP || js.tris.length > TCAP || js.groups.length > GCAP) { over++; continue; }
        const vt = cntV[0], tt = cntV[1], gc = cntV[2], cons = cntV[3];
        let why = null;
        if (ret !== 0) why = `ret ${ret}`;
        else if (vt !== js.pos.length) why = `vcount ${vt}!=${js.pos.length}`;
        else if (tt !== js.tris.length) why = `tcount ${tt}!=${js.tris.length}`;
        else if (gc !== js.groups.length) why = `gcount ${gc}!=${js.groups.length}`;
        else if (cons !== js.consumed) why = `consumed ${cons}!=${js.consumed}`;
        if (!why) {
          const jp = new Float32Array(vt * 3), jn = new Float32Array(vt * 3), ju = new Float32Array(vt * 2), jt = new Uint32Array(tt * 3);
          for (let i = 0; i < vt; i++) { jp[i*3]=js.pos[i][0]; jp[i*3+1]=js.pos[i][1]; jp[i*3+2]=js.pos[i][2];
            jn[i*3]=js.nrm[i][0]; jn[i*3+1]=js.nrm[i][1]; jn[i*3+2]=js.nrm[i][2]; ju[i*2]=js.uv[i][0]; ju[i*2+1]=js.uv[i][1]; }
          for (let i = 0; i < tt; i++) { jt[i*3]=js.tris[i][0]; jt[i*3+1]=js.tris[i][1]; jt[i*3+2]=js.tris[i][2]; }
          let bi;
          if ((bi = eqF(posV, jp, vt*3)) >= 0) why = `pos[${bi}]`;
          else if ((bi = eqF(nrmV, jn, vt*3)) >= 0) why = `nrm[${bi}]`;
          else if ((bi = eqF(uvV, ju, vt*2)) >= 0) why = `uv[${bi}]`;
          else if ((bi = eqF(triV, jt, tt*3)) >= 0) why = `tri[${bi}]`;
          else for (let g = 0; g < gc && !why; g++) if (grpV[g*2] !== js.groups[g].triStart || grpV[g*2+1] !== js.groups[g].triEnd) why = `grp[${g}]`;
        }
        if (why) { bad++; firstBad = firstBad || `${t.name}: ${why}`; } else ok++;
      }
      console.log(`\nshp mesh decode: ${ok}/${toc.length} meshes byte-identical to web/shp.js` +
        ` (${rej} rejected by both as non-mesh${over ? `, ${over} over cap` : ''}${bad ? `, ${bad} MISMATCH` : ''})`);
      if (firstBad) console.log(`  first mismatch: ${firstBad}`);
      fails += bad;
    }
  }

  // ---- axis_bfd_extract: WASM map decoder vs web/bfd.js over every Map/*.bfd
  const mapDir2 = path.join(game, 'Map');
  if (typeof bfdExtract === 'function' && fs.existsSync(mapDir2)) {
    let MAP;
    try { MAP = require(path.join(__dirname, '..', '..', 'web', 'bfd.js')); }
    catch (e) { console.log('bfd.js not loadable — skipping map check:', e.message); MAP = null; }
    if (MAP) {
      const BF = BASE, BP = 0x1400000, BC = 0x1480000, OCAP = 50000;   // file, pos-out, counts
      ensure(BC + 64);
      let ok = 0, bad = 0, nmaps = 0, firstBad = null;
      for (const fn of fs.readdirSync(mapDir2).filter(f => /\.bfd$/i.test(f))) {
        const raw = fs.readFileSync(path.join(mapDir2, fn));
        const ab = raw.buffer.slice(raw.byteOffset, raw.byteOffset + raw.byteLength);
        const js = MAP.extractMap(ab);                       // JS: decrypt + parse
        if (js.objects.length > OCAP) { continue; }
        new Uint8Array(memory.buffer).set(new Uint8Array(ab), BF);   // WASM decrypts in place
        bfdExtract(BF, raw.length, BP, BC);
        const cnt = new Uint32Array(memory.buffer, BC, 3);
        const posV = new Float32Array(memory.buffer, BP, js.objects.length * 3);
        nmaps++;
        let why = null;
        const jsVer = parseInt(js.version, 16) >>> 0;
        if (cnt[0] !== jsVer) why = `version ${cnt[0].toString(16)}!=${js.version}`;
        else if (cnt[1] !== js.count) why = `count ${cnt[1]}!=${js.count}`;
        else if (cnt[2] !== js.objects.length) why = `emitted ${cnt[2]}!=${js.objects.length}`;
        else for (let i = 0; i < js.objects.length && !why; i++) {
          const p = js.objects[i].pos;
          if (!Object.is(posV[i*3], p[0]) || !Object.is(posV[i*3+1], p[1]) || !Object.is(posV[i*3+2], p[2]))
            why = `${fn} obj[${i}] pos`;
        }
        if (why) { bad++; firstBad = firstBad || `${fn}: ${why}`; } else ok++;
      }
      console.log(`\nbfd map decode : ${ok}/${nmaps} maps byte-identical to web/bfd.js (version+count+all object positions)${bad ? `, ${bad} MISMATCH` : ''}`);
      if (firstBad) console.log(`  first mismatch: ${firstBad}`);
      fails += bad;
    }
  }

  // ---- axis_ptx_decode: WASM texture decoder vs web/ptx.js over real Image*.dat PTX entries
  const exeFile = path.join(game, 'AXIS.EXE');
  if (typeof ptxDecode === 'function' && fs.existsSync(exeFile)) {
    let PTX, SHP2;
    try { PTX = require(path.join(__dirname, '..', '..', 'web', 'ptx.js')); SHP2 = require(path.join(__dirname, '..', '..', 'web', 'shp.js')); }
    catch (e) { console.log('ptx.js/shp.js not loadable — skipping texture check:', e.message); PTX = null; }
    if (PTX) {
      const exeRaw = fs.readFileSync(exeFile);
      const exeAb = exeRaw.buffer.slice(exeRaw.byteOffset, exeRaw.byteOffset + exeRaw.byteLength);
      const EXEB = 0x1000000, PTXB = 0x1800000, RGBAB = 0x1900000, WHB = 0x1E00000;
      ensure(WHB + 64);
      new Uint8Array(memory.buffer).set(new Uint8Array(exeAb), EXEB);
      const exeU8 = new Uint8Array(exeAb);
      let ok = 0, bad = 0, nonptx = 0, tested = 0, firstBad = null;
      const PER = 15;                                   // sample per Image archive (keeps JS runtime sane)
      for (let n = 0; n < 6; n++) {
        const dat = path.join(game, 'Data', `Image${n}.dat`);
        if (!fs.existsSync(dat)) continue;
        const raw = fs.readFileSync(dat);
        const ab = raw.buffer.slice(raw.byteOffset, raw.byteOffset + raw.byteLength);
        const toc = SHP2.readTOC(ab);
        for (let i = 0; i < toc.length && i < PER; i++) {
          const t = toc[i];
          const slice = ab.slice(t.off, t.end);
          const sU8 = new Uint8Array(slice);
          if (!(sU8[0] === 0x50 && sU8[1] === 0x54 && sU8[2] === 0x58 && sU8[3] === 0)) { nonptx++; continue; }
          let js;
          try { js = PTX.decodePTX(slice, exeU8); } catch (e) { nonptx++; continue; }
          if (js.w * js.h > 1024 * 1024) continue;
          new Uint8Array(memory.buffer).set(sU8, PTXB);
          const ret = ptxDecode(PTXB, sU8.length, EXEB, exeU8.length, RGBAB, WHB);
          tested++;
          const wh = new Uint32Array(memory.buffer, WHB, 2);
          let why = null;
          if (ret !== 0) why = `ret ${ret}`;
          else if (wh[0] !== js.w || wh[1] !== js.h) why = `dims ${wh[0]}x${wh[1]}!=${js.w}x${js.h}`;
          else {
            const got = new Uint8Array(memory.buffer, RGBAB, js.w * js.h * 4);
            const jr = js.rgba;
            for (let k = 0; k < jr.length; k++) if (got[k] !== jr[k]) { why = `rgba[${k}] ${got[k]}!=${jr[k]}`; break; }
          }
          if (why) { bad++; firstBad = firstBad || `Image${n}/${t.name}: ${why}`; } else ok++;
        }
      }
      console.log(`\nptx texture decode: ${ok}/${tested} textures byte-identical to web/ptx.js (${nonptx} non-PTX entries skipped)${bad ? `, ${bad} MISMATCH` : ''}`);
      if (firstBad) console.log(`  first mismatch: ${firstBad}`);
      fails += bad;
    }
  }

  // ---- axis_skel_parse: WASM bone decoder vs web/skel.js over every Skeleton.dat rig
  const skelDat = path.join(game, 'Data', 'Skeleton.dat');
  if (typeof skelParse === 'function' && fs.existsSync(skelDat)) {
    let SK;
    try { SK = require(path.join(__dirname, '..', '..', 'web', 'skel.js')); }
    catch (e) { console.log('skel.js not loadable — skipping skeleton check:', e.message); SK = null; }
    if (SK) {
      const raw = fs.readFileSync(skelDat);
      const ab = raw.buffer.slice(raw.byteOffset, raw.byteOffset + raw.byteLength);
      const list = SK.listSkeletons(ab);
      const SF = BASE, PAR = 0x1400000, FOUT = 0x1440000, SC = 0x14C0000, BCAP = 8192;
      ensure(SC + 64);
      new Uint8Array(memory.buffer).set(new Uint8Array(ab), SF);
      let ok = 0, bad = 0, firstBad = null;
      for (const e of list) {
        const js = SK.parseSkeleton(ab, e);
        if (js.bones.length > BCAP) continue;
        skelParse(SF, e.offset, e.size, PAR, FOUT, SC);
        const cnt = new Uint32Array(memory.buffer, SC, 2);
        const par = new Int32Array(memory.buffer, PAR, js.bones.length);
        const fo = new Float32Array(memory.buffer, FOUT, js.bones.length * 7);
        let why = null;
        if (cnt[1] !== js.bones.length) why = `emitted ${cnt[1]}!=${js.bones.length}`;
        else for (let b = 0; b < js.bones.length && !why; b++) {
          const J = js.bones[b];
          if (par[b] !== J.parent) why = `bone[${b}].parent`;
          else if (!Object.is(fo[b*7], J.t[0]) || !Object.is(fo[b*7+1], J.t[1]) || !Object.is(fo[b*7+2], J.t[2])) why = `bone[${b}].t`;
          else if (!Object.is(fo[b*7+3], J.angle)) why = `bone[${b}].angle`;
          else if (!Object.is(fo[b*7+4], J.axis[0]) || !Object.is(fo[b*7+5], J.axis[1]) || !Object.is(fo[b*7+6], J.axis[2])) why = `bone[${b}].axis`;
        }
        if (why) { bad++; firstBad = firstBad || `${e.name}: ${why}`; } else ok++;
      }
      console.log(`\nskeleton decode: ${ok}/${list.length} rigs byte-identical to web/skel.js (parent+translation+axis-angle)${bad ? `, ${bad} MISMATCH` : ''}`);
      if (firstBad) console.log(`  first mismatch: ${firstBad}`);
      fails += bad;
    }
  }

  // ---- axis_ape_parse: WASM replay decoder vs web/ape.js over every AutoPlay/*.ape
  const apDir2 = path.join(game, 'AutoPlay');
  if (typeof apeParse === 'function' && fs.existsSync(apDir2)) {
    let AP;
    try { AP = require(path.join(__dirname, '..', '..', 'web', 'ape.js')); }
    catch (e) { console.log('ape.js not loadable — skipping replay check:', e.message); AP = null; }
    if (AP) {
      const AF = BASE, ROW = 0x1400000, YAW = 0x1600000, AC = 0x1700000;
      ensure(AC + 64);
      let ok = 0, bad = 0, nfiles = 0, firstBad = null;
      for (const fn of fs.readdirSync(apDir2).filter(f => /\.ape$/i.test(f))) {
        const raw = fs.readFileSync(path.join(apDir2, fn));
        const ab = raw.buffer.slice(raw.byteOffset, raw.byteOffset + raw.byteLength);
        const js = AP.parseReplay(ab);
        new Uint8Array(memory.buffer).set(new Uint8Array(ab), AF);
        apeParse(AF, raw.length, ROW, YAW, AC);
        const cnt = new Uint32Array(memory.buffer, AC, 3);
        const nrows = cnt[2];
        const rows = new Int32Array(memory.buffer, ROW, nrows * 5);
        const yaws = new Float64Array(memory.buffer, YAW, nrows);
        nfiles++;
        // rebuild per-actor tracks from WASM rows (record order) and compare to ape.js tracks
        const wt = {};
        for (let r = 0; r < nrows; r++) { const a = rows[r*5]; (wt[a] || (wt[a] = [])).push([rows[r*5+1], rows[r*5+2], rows[r*5+3], rows[r*5+4], yaws[r]]); }
        let why = null;
        const jsStart = js.start, wStart = (cnt[0] === 0xffffffff) ? -1 : cnt[0];
        if (wStart !== jsStart) why = `start ${wStart}!=${jsStart}`;
        else if (cnt[1] !== js.records) why = `records ${cnt[1]}!=${js.records}`;
        else {
          const acts = Object.keys(js.tracks);
          for (const a of acts) {
            const jt = js.tracks[a], w = wt[a] || [];
            if (w.length !== jt.length) { why = `actor ${a} len ${w.length}!=${jt.length}`; break; }
            for (let i = 0; i < jt.length; i++) {
              if (w[i][0] !== jt[i][0] || w[i][1] !== jt[i][1] || w[i][2] !== jt[i][2] || w[i][3] !== jt[i][3] || !Object.is(w[i][4], jt[i][4])) { why = `actor ${a} row ${i}`; break; }
            }
            if (why) break;
          }
          if (!why && Object.keys(wt).length !== acts.length) why = `actor count ${Object.keys(wt).length}!=${acts.length}`;
        }
        if (why) { bad++; firstBad = firstBad || `${fn}: ${why}`; } else ok++;
      }
      console.log(`\nape replay decode: ${ok}/${nfiles} replays byte-identical to web/ape.js (start+records+all type-0 trajectories)${bad ? `, ${bad} MISMATCH` : ''}`);
      if (firstBad) console.log(`  first mismatch: ${firstBad}`);
      fails += bad;
    }
  }

  console.log(fails ? `\nFAILED (${fails})` : '\nALL CHECKS PASS (emcc toolchain build verified against real game data)');
  process.exit(fails ? 1 : 0);
}
main().catch(e => { console.error(e); process.exit(1); });

```

`run.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""One-shot local runner for the AXIS web explorer.

    python run.py                # auto-detects the game dir (../Axis_Win)
    python run.py <game_dir>     # or point it at your AXIS install
    python run.py <game_dir> -p 8123

Builds web/catalog.json from your installed game, serves web/ on localhost,
and opens the browser. Nothing is uploaded; the catalog holds metadata only.
"""
import sys
import os
import subprocess
import webbrowser
import http.server
import socketserver

HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
WEB = os.path.join(HERE, "web")


def find_game(argv):
    for a in argv:
        if not a.startswith("-") and os.path.isdir(a):
            return os.path.abspath(a)
    # default guesses, relative to this repo
    for cand in (os.path.join(HERE, "..", "Axis_Win"),
                 os.path.join(HERE, "..", "axis", "Axis_Win"),
                 os.path.join(HERE, "game")):
        if os.path.isdir(os.path.join(cand, "Data")):
            return os.path.abspath(cand)
    return None


def main():
    port = 8123
    if "-p" in sys.argv:
        port = int(sys.argv[sys.argv.index("-p") + 1])

    game = find_game(sys.argv[1:])
    if not game:
        print("Could not find your AXIS install. Pass it explicitly:\n"
              "    python run.py /path/to/Axis_Win")
        return 1
    print(f"game dir : {game}")

    out_json = os.path.join(WEB, "catalog.json")
    rc = subprocess.call([sys.executable, "-I",
                          os.path.join(HERE, "tools", "build_catalog.py"),
                          game, out_json])
    if rc != 0:
        return rc

    # extract every map's object layout for the 3D viewer
    maps_dir = os.path.join(WEB, "maps")
    os.makedirs(maps_dir, exist_ok=True)
    src_maps = os.path.join(game, "Map")
    index = []
    if os.path.isdir(src_maps):
        import json
        for fn in sorted(os.listdir(src_maps)):
            if not fn.lower().endswith(".bfd"):
                continue
            name = os.path.splitext(fn)[0]
            subprocess.call([sys.executable, "-I",
                             os.path.join(HERE, "tools", "extract_map.py"),
                             os.path.join(src_maps, fn),
                             os.path.join(maps_dir, name + ".json")],
                            stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL)
            index.append(name)
        with open(os.path.join(maps_dir, "index.json"), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            json.dump(index, f)
        print(f"maps     : {len(index)} map layouts -> web/maps/")

    # export mesh geometry from Shape.dat for the 3D mesh viewer
    shape_dat = os.path.join(game, "Data", "Shape.dat")
    if os.path.isfile(shape_dat):
        subprocess.call([sys.executable, "-I",
                         os.path.join(HERE, "tools", "export_shapes.py"),
                         shape_dat, os.path.join(WEB, "shapes")])

    # export bind-pose skeletons from Skeleton.dat for the skeleton viewer
    skel_dat = os.path.join(game, "Data", "Skeleton.dat")
    if os.path.isfile(skel_dat):
        subprocess.call([sys.executable, "-I",
                         os.path.join(HERE, "tools", "parse_skeleton.py"),
                         skel_dat, os.path.join(WEB, "skeletons")])

    # export recorded match replays (AutoPlay/*.ape) for the replay viewer
    ap_dir = os.path.join(game, "AutoPlay")
    if os.path.isdir(ap_dir):
        import json
        rep_dir = os.path.join(WEB, "replays")
        os.makedirs(rep_dir, exist_ok=True)
        rindex = []
        for fn in sorted(os.listdir(ap_dir)):
            if not fn.lower().endswith(".ape"):
                continue
            base = os.path.splitext(fn)[0]
            rc = subprocess.call([sys.executable, "-I",
                                  os.path.join(HERE, "tools", "parse_ape.py"),
                                  os.path.join(ap_dir, fn),
                                  os.path.join(rep_dir, base + ".json")],
                                 stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL)
            if rc == 0:
                rindex.append({"name": base, "file": base + ".json"})
        if rindex:
            with open(os.path.join(rep_dir, "index.json"), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                json.dump(rindex, f)
            print(f"replays  : {len(rindex)} recorded matches -> web/replays/")

    # index the loose audio (Sound/*.wav, Music/*.mp3) for the audio browser.
    # Only filenames are written to web/audio.json; the audio is served live
    # from the user's install via /game/ (nothing is copied or committed).
    import json
    audio = {"music": [], "sfx": []}
    music_dir = os.path.join(game, "Music")
    if os.path.isdir(music_dir):
        for fn in sorted(os.listdir(music_dir)):
            if fn.lower().endswith((".mp3", ".ogg", ".wav")):
                audio["music"].append("Music/" + fn)
    sound_dir = os.path.join(game, "Sound")
    if os.path.isdir(sound_dir):
        for root, _dirs, files in os.walk(sound_dir):
            for fn in sorted(files):
                if fn.lower().endswith((".wav", ".mp3", ".ogg")):
                    rel = os.path.relpath(os.path.join(root, fn), game).replace(os.sep, "/")
                    audio["sfx"].append(rel)
    if audio["music"] or audio["sfx"]:
        with open(os.path.join(WEB, "audio.json"), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            json.dump(audio, f)
        print(f"audio    : {len(audio['music'])} tracks + {len(audio['sfx'])} sfx -> web/audio.json")

    # Decode textures (PTX -> PNG) for the gallery fallback IN THE BACKGROUND, so the
    # server comes up immediately: every viewer decodes textures live from /game/ via
    # ptx.js, and the pre-rendered PNGs are only the offline fallback for textures.html.
    if os.path.isfile(os.path.join(game, "AXIS.EXE")):
        def _bake_textures():
            subprocess.call([sys.executable, "-I",
                             os.path.join(HERE, "tools", "export_textures.py"),
                             game, os.path.join(WEB, "textures")],
                            stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL)
            print("textures : gallery PNGs baked -> web/textures/ (live decode via /game/ meanwhile)")
        import threading
        threading.Thread(target=_bake_textures, daemon=True).start()

    # serve web/ normally, plus /game/<path> from the user's install (read-only, local)
    # so the browser can decode textures/meshes live from their own game data.
    class Handler(http.server.SimpleHTTPRequestHandler):
        def translate_path(self, path):
            p = path.split("?", 1)[0].split("#", 1)[0]
            if p.startswith("/game/"):
                rel = p[len("/game/"):].lstrip("/")
                parts = [x for x in rel.split("/") if x not in ("", ".", "..")]
                return os.path.join(game, *parts)
            return super().translate_path(path)

        def log_message(self, *a):
            pass

        def __init__(self, *a, **k):
            super().__init__(*a, directory=WEB, **k)

    socketserver.TCPServer.allow_reuse_address = True
    with socketserver.TCPServer(("127.0.0.1", port), Handler) as httpd:
        url = f"http://127.0.0.1:{port}/home.html"
        print(f"serving  : {url}  (Ctrl+C to stop)", flush=True)
        try:
            webbrowser.open(url)
        except Exception:
            pass
        try:
            httpd.serve_forever()
        except KeyboardInterrupt:
            print("\nstopped.")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

```

`server/README.md`:

```md
# AXIS multiplayer relay (server skeleton)

A zero-dependency Node.js WebSocket server that reproduces the reverse-engineered AXIS
packet transport (see `../docs/SPEC.md §4`). It is the server the browser/WASM client
connects to in place of the original Winsock server.

```sh
node relay.js [port]      # default 8140, no npm install needed
```

## What it does (verified)

- WebSocket handshake + framing using only Node's `http`/`crypto` (no `ws` dependency).
- Assigns each client an **actor slot** (0–15, matching AXIS's 0x10 actor loop) and tells
  it its slot with a `[0xFF, actorId]` control message.
- Accepts the game's **fixed 64-byte player-action payload** and **relays** it to the other
  clients, framed as `[u32 tick][u8 actorId][64-byte payload]` so clients can order and
  attribute updates. (AXIS uses a per-channel sequenced relay model.)

A two-client self-test confirms a 64-byte packet sent by one client arrives at the other as
a 69-byte relayed frame with the correct tick, source actor, and payload.

## What it is not (yet)

This is the **transport + slot + relay** foundation, not full game logic. Completing the
server needs every packet type's semantics and server-side validation — which are
recoverable from **decrypted `.ape` replays** (they record the real 64-byte packets, see
`../docs/SPEC.md §4`) plus reversing the recv/dispatch path. Those build directly on top of
this relay.

```

`server/relay.js`:

```js
#!/usr/bin/env node
/*
 * AXIS multiplayer relay — a zero-dependency WebSocket server that reproduces the
 * reverse-engineered packet model (see ../docs/SPEC.md §4):
 *
 *   - clients exchange fixed 64-byte player-action payloads (the game's packet body),
 *   - the server assigns each client an actor slot and relays every packet it receives
 *     to the other clients (the game uses a per-channel sequenced relay model),
 *   - a monotonically increasing tick is stamped on each relayed frame.
 *
 * This is the server skeleton the WASM client (approach B, ../port) connects to in place
 * of the original Winsock server. Full gameplay needs every packet type's semantics
 * (recoverable from decrypted .ape replays, §4) and server-side validation; this gives the
 * transport + slot assignment + relay that all of that builds on.
 *
 * No npm install — the WebSocket handshake and framing use only Node's http/crypto.
 *     node relay.js [port]        (default 8140)
 */
'use strict';
const http = require('http');
const crypto = require('crypto');

const PORT = parseInt(process.argv[2] || '8140', 10);
const PAYLOAD = 0x40;                 // 64-byte player-action payload (SPEC §4)
const MAX_PLAYERS = 16;               // AXIS iterates 0x10 actor slots
const WS_GUID = '258EAFA5-E914-47DA-95CA-C5AB0DC85B11';

const clients = new Map();            // actorId -> { socket, alive }
let tick = 0;

function accept(key) {
  return crypto.createHash('sha1').update(key + WS_GUID).digest('base64');
}

function wsFrame(payload) {           // server->client binary frame (unmasked)
  const len = payload.length;
  let head;
  if (len < 126) head = Buffer.from([0x82, len]);
  else if (len < 65536) { head = Buffer.alloc(4); head[0] = 0x82; head[1] = 126; head.writeUInt16BE(len, 2); }
  else { head = Buffer.alloc(10); head[0] = 0x82; head[1] = 127; head.writeUInt32BE(0, 2); head.writeUInt32BE(len, 6); }
  return Buffer.concat([head, payload]);
}

function send(sock, payload) { try { sock.write(wsFrame(payload)); } catch (e) {} }

function firstFreeSlot() {
  for (let i = 0; i < MAX_PLAYERS; i++) if (!clients.has(i)) return i;
  return -1;
}

// relay one 64-byte player payload from `fromId` to everyone else, framed as
// [u32 tick][u8 actor][payload...] so the client can order and attribute it.
function relay(fromId, payload) {
  const frame = Buffer.alloc(5 + payload.length);
  frame.writeUInt32LE(tick++, 0);
  frame.writeUInt8(fromId, 4);
  payload.copy(frame, 5);
  for (const [id, c] of clients) if (id !== fromId) send(c.socket, frame);
}

function parseFrames(buf, onMsg) {     // minimal client->server frame parser
  let off = 0;
  while (off + 2 <= buf.length) {
    const b1 = buf[off], b2 = buf[off + 1];
    const opcode = b1 & 0x0f, masked = b2 & 0x80;
    let len = b2 & 0x7f, p = off + 2;
    if (len === 126) { if (p + 2 > buf.length) break; len = buf.readUInt16BE(p); p += 2; }
    else if (len === 127) { if (p + 8 > buf.length) break; len = Number(buf.readBigUInt64BE(p)); p += 8; }
    let mask = null;
    if (masked) { if (p + 4 > buf.length) break; mask = buf.slice(p, p + 4); p += 4; }
    if (p + len > buf.length) break;
    const data = buf.slice(p, p + len);
    if (mask) for (let i = 0; i < data.length; i++) data[i] ^= mask[i & 3];
    if (opcode === 0x8) return { closed: true, rest: buf.slice(p + len) };
    if (opcode === 0x1 || opcode === 0x2) onMsg(data);
    off = p + len;
  }
  return { rest: buf.slice(off) };
}

const server = http.createServer((req, res) => { res.writeHead(426); res.end('WebSocket only'); });

server.on('upgrade', (req, socket) => {
  const key = req.headers['sec-websocket-key'];
  if (!key) { socket.destroy(); return; }
  socket.write('HTTP/1.1 101 Switching Protocols\r\n' +
    'Upgrade: websocket\r\nConnection: Upgrade\r\n' +
    'Sec-WebSocket-Accept: ' + accept(key) + '\r\n\r\n');

  const id = firstFreeSlot();
  if (id < 0) { socket.destroy(); return; }
  clients.set(id, { socket });
  console.log(`+ player ${id} joined (${clients.size}/${MAX_PLAYERS})`);
  // tell the client its assigned actor slot: [0xFF][actorId]
  send(socket, Buffer.from([0xff, id]));

  let acc = Buffer.alloc(0);
  socket.on('data', (chunk) => {
    acc = Buffer.concat([acc, chunk]);
    const r = parseFrames(acc, (data) => {
      // accept the fixed 64-byte payload (ignore anything malformed)
      if (data.length === PAYLOAD) relay(id, data);
    });
    acc = r.rest;
    if (r.closed) socket.end();
  });
  const bye = () => { if (clients.delete(id)) console.log(`- player ${id} left (${clients.size}/${MAX_PLAYERS})`); };
  socket.on('close', bye); socket.on('error', bye);
});

server.listen(PORT, () => {
  console.log(`AXIS relay on ws://127.0.0.1:${PORT}  (64-byte packets, ${MAX_PLAYERS} slots)`);
});

```

`tools/bfd_crypt.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""AXIS obfuscation codec — used by the encrypted data files
(`Map/*.bfd`, `*.ape`, `Data/Item.dat`, `Data/Component.dat`, `Data/Sound.dat`).

The decrypting read `FUN_0046c060` does, per byte, after a normal fread:
    b = rotate_left_8(b ^ 0x59, 1)
The transform is position-independent, so decrypting a whole buffer uniformly
equals the game's per-read decryption.

Most files keep a 4-byte PLAINTEXT version prefix (0x1201 / 0x1204) read by the
