```html
<script src="shp.js"></script>
<script>
const cv=document.getElementById("c"),ctx=cv.getContext("2d"),hud=document.getElementById("hud");
let DPR=Math.min(devicePixelRatio||1,2);
let M=null, center=[0,0,0], ext=1, yaw=0.6, pitch=0.4, dist=2.3, spin=true, wire=false, raf=0;

function resize(){DPR=Math.min(devicePixelRatio||1,2);cv.width=cv.clientWidth*DPR;cv.height=cv.clientHeight*DPR;}
addEventListener("resize",()=>{resize();draw();});

function rot(p){
  let x=p[0]-center[0],y=p[1]-center[1],z=p[2]-center[2];
  const cy=Math.cos(yaw),sy=Math.sin(yaw); let x1=x*cy+z*sy,z1=-x*sy+z*cy;
  const cp=Math.cos(pitch),sp=Math.sin(pitch); let y2=y*cp-z1*sp,z2=y*sp+z1*cp;
  return [x1,y2,z2];
}
function proj(r){ const depth=r[2]+ext*dist; if(depth<=1)return null;
  const f=Math.min(cv.width,cv.height)*0.9; return [cv.width/2+r[0]*f/depth, cv.height/2-r[1]*f/depth, depth]; }

function draw(){
  const css=getComputedStyle(document.documentElement);
  ctx.fillStyle=css.getPropertyValue("--bg"); ctx.fillRect(0,0,cv.width,cv.height);
  if(!M) return;
  const P=M.pos.map(rot), S=P.map(proj);
  // light direction (view space)
  const L=[0.4,0.6,-0.68];
  const faces=[];
  for(const t of M.tris){
    const a=S[t[0]],b=S[t[1]],c=S[t[2]]; if(!a||!b||!c) continue;
    const z=(a[2]+b[2]+c[2])/3;
    // face normal from rotated positions (for shading, independent of stored normals)
    const ra=P[t[0]],rb=P[t[1]],rc=P[t[2]];
    const u=[rb[0]-ra[0],rb[1]-ra[1],rb[2]-ra[2]], v=[rc[0]-ra[0],rc[1]-ra[1],rc[2]-ra[2]];
    let nx=u[1]*v[2]-u[2]*v[1], ny=u[2]*v[0]-u[0]*v[2], nz=u[0]*v[1]-u[1]*v[0];
    const ln=Math.hypot(nx,ny,nz)||1; nx/=ln;ny/=ln;nz/=ln;
    let d=nx*L[0]+ny*L[1]+nz*L[2]; d=Math.abs(d); // two-sided
    faces.push([z,a,b,c,d]);
  }
  faces.sort((p,q)=>q[0]-p[0]);
  const acc=css.getPropertyValue("--accent").trim();
  for(const [z,a,b,c,d] of faces){
    const sh=Math.round(40+d*175);
    ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(a[0],a[1]);ctx.lineTo(b[0],b[1]);ctx.lineTo(c[0],c[1]);ctx.closePath();
    if(wire){ ctx.strokeStyle=acc; ctx.globalAlpha=0.5; ctx.lineWidth=DPR; ctx.stroke(); ctx.globalAlpha=1; }
    else { ctx.fillStyle=`rgb(${Math.round(sh*0.55)},${Math.round(sh*0.78)},${sh})`; ctx.fill();
           ctx.strokeStyle=`rgb(${Math.round(sh*0.3)},${Math.round(sh*0.45)},${Math.round(sh*0.6)})`;
           ctx.lineWidth=0.5*DPR; ctx.stroke(); }
  }
}
function loop(){ if(spin){yaw+=0.006; draw();} raf=requestAnimationFrame(loop); }

function fit(){
  const mn=[1e9,1e9,1e9],mx=[-1e9,-1e9,-1e9];
  for(const p of M.pos)for(let k=0;k<3;k++){mn[k]=Math.min(mn[k],p[k]);mx[k]=Math.max(mx[k],p[k]);}
  center=[(mn[0]+mx[0])/2,(mn[1]+mx[1])/2,(mn[2]+mx[2])/2];
  ext=Math.max(1,Math.max(mx[0]-mn[0],mx[1]-mn[1],mx[2]-mn[2]));
  hud.textContent=`size ${(mx[0]-mn[0])|0} × ${(mx[1]-mn[1])|0} × ${(mx[2]-mn[2])|0}`;
}
function load(name){ fetch("shapes/"+name+".json").then(r=>r.json()).then(d=>{
  M=d; fit();
  document.getElementById("stats").innerHTML=
    `<span>shape</span><b>${d.name}</b><span>vertices</span><b>${d.pos.length}</b><span>triangles</span><b>${d.tris.length}</b>`;
  draw(); try{localStorage.setItem("axshape",name);}catch(e){}
});}

let drag=null;
cv.addEventListener("pointerdown",e=>{drag={x:e.clientX,y:e.clientY};cv.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);});
cv.addEventListener("pointermove",e=>{ if(!drag)return; yaw-=(e.clientX-drag.x)*0.01;
  pitch=Math.max(-1.4,Math.min(1.4,pitch+(e.clientY-drag.y)*0.01)); drag.x=e.clientX;drag.y=e.clientY; if(!spin)draw(); });
cv.addEventListener("pointerup",()=>drag=null);
cv.addEventListener("wheel",e=>{e.preventDefault();dist=Math.max(0.6,Math.min(8,dist*(e.deltaY>0?1.1:0.9)));if(!spin)draw();},{passive:false});
document.getElementById("wire").addEventListener("change",e=>{wire=e.target.checked;draw();});
document.getElementById("spin").addEventListener("change",e=>{spin=e.target.checked;});

// Live mode: decode meshes in-browser straight from Shape.dat (shp.js), no pre-export.
async function initLive(){
  const ab=await (await fetch("/game/Data/Shape.dat")).arrayBuffer();
  const toc=window.AXISShape.readTOC(ab);
  const sel=document.getElementById("sel");
  sel.innerHTML=toc.map((e,i)=>`<option value="${i}">${e.name}</option>`).join("");
  function loadIdx(i){ const e=toc[i];
    try{ const r=window.AXISShape.parseShape(ab,e.off,e.end);
      if(!r.pos.length||!r.tris.length){hud.textContent="(empty mesh)";return;}
      M={name:e.name,pos:r.pos,nrm:r.nrm,uv:r.uv,tris:r.tris}; fit();
      document.getElementById("stats").innerHTML=
        `<span>shape</span><b>${e.name}</b><span>vertices</span><b>${r.pos.length}</b><span>triangles</span><b>${r.tris.length}</b>`;
      draw(); try{localStorage.setItem("axshapei",i);}catch(_){}
    }catch(err){ hud.textContent="decode: "+err.message; }
  }
  let start=toc.findIndex(e=>e.name.toUpperCase().endsWith("GIRL")); if(start<0)start=0;
  try{const s=+localStorage.getItem("axshapei"); if(s>=0&&s<toc.length)start=s;}catch(e){}
  sel.value=start; sel.addEventListener("change",()=>loadIdx(+sel.value));
  document.querySelector(".sub").textContent=`live from Shape.dat · ${toc.length} meshes (shp.js)`;
  resize(); loadIdx(start); loop();
}
// fall back to the pre-exported JSON (python run.py) if /game/ isn't served
function initJSON(){
  fetch("shapes/index.json").then(r=>r.json()).then(list=>{
    const items=list.map(e=>typeof e==="string"?{id:e,name:e,v:0,t:0}:e);
    const ids=items.map(e=>e.id);
    const sel=document.getElementById("sel");
    sel.innerHTML=items.map(e=>`<option value="${e.id}">${e.name}${e.t?`  (${e.t} tris)`:""}</option>`).join("");
    let start=ids.includes("ALIEN_GIRL")?"ALIEN_GIRL":ids[0];
    try{const s=localStorage.getItem("axshape"); if(s&&ids.includes(s))start=s;}catch(e){}
    sel.value=start; sel.addEventListener("change",()=>load(sel.value));
    document.querySelector(".sub").textContent=`real geometry from Shape.dat · ${items.length} meshes`;
    resize(); load(start); loop();
  }).catch(err=>{ resize(); hud.textContent="Run `python run.py` first."; });
}
fetch("/game/Data/Shape.dat",{method:"HEAD"}).then(r=>{ if(r.ok&&window.AXISShape) initLive(); else initJSON(); }).catch(initJSON);
</script>
</body>
</html>
```

```html
<!doctype html>
<html lang="en">
<head>

<title>AXIS Textured Mesh</title>
<style>

    --fg:#e6edf6; --muted:#8aa0bd; --accent:#38d0c8; color-scheme:dark;

  *{box-sizing:border-box} html,body{height:100%}
  body{margin:0;background:var(--bg);color:var(--fg);font:14px/1.5 var(--sans);overflow:hidden}
  .wrap{display:grid;grid-template-columns:260px 1fr;height:100%}
  @media (max-width:720px){ .wrap{grid-template-columns:1fr;grid-template-rows:auto 1fr} }

  .stat b{color:var(--fg);font-weight:600}
  .row{display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:center;font:12px/1 var(--sans);color:var(--muted)}
  input[type=checkbox]{accent-color:var(--accent)}
  .hint{color:var(--muted);font-size:11px;line-height:1.5} a{color:var(--accent)}

  canvas#c{display:block;width:100%;height:100%;touch-action:none;cursor:grab}
  canvas#c:active{cursor:grabbing}
  .hud{position:absolute;left:12px;top:12px;font:12px/1.4 var(--mono);color:var(--muted);
    background:color-mix(in srgb,var(--panel) 80%,transparent);padding:6px 9px;border-radius:7px;border:1px solid var(--line);pointer-events:none}
  .tex{position:absolute;right:12px;bottom:12px;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:6px;image-rendering:pixelated;width:96px;height:96px;background:var(--panel2)}
</style>
</head>
<body>
<div class="wrap">
  <aside class="side">
    <div><h1>AXIS <span>Textured Mesh</span></h1><div class="sub">real skins applied to the meshes</div></div>
    <div><label for="sel">Shape</label><select id="sel"></select></div>
    <div class="stat" id="stats"></div>
    <label class="row"><input type="checkbox" id="tex" checked> texture</label>
    <label class="row"><input type="checkbox" id="spin" checked> auto-rotate</label>
    <div class="hint">Each mesh is drawn with its real texture, decoded in-browser by
      <code>ptx.js</code> from <code>Image*.dat</code> and mapped with the mesh's own UVs
      (the texture is the shape's <code>SHAPE\…</code> skin — SPEC §2.9). Drag to orbit · wheel zoom.</div>
    <div class="hint"><a href="shape.html">← mesh viewer</a> · <a href="home.html">home →</a></div>
  </aside>
  <div class="view"><canvas id="c"></canvas><canvas class="tex" id="texview" width="96" height="96"></canvas><div class="hud" id="hud"></div></div>
</div>
<script src="shp.js"></script>
<script src="ptx.js"></script>
<script>
const cv=document.getElementById("c"),ctx=cv.getContext("2d"),hud=document.getElementById("hud");
let DPR=Math.min(devicePixelRatio||1,2);
let M=null, TEX=null, center=[0,0,0], ext=1, yaw=0.6, pitch=0.35, dist=2.3, spin=true, useTex=true, raf=0;
let EXE=null, SHAPE=null, SHAPES=[], IMG=[];   // IMG: [{toc, ab}] per Image*.dat

function resize(){DPR=Math.min(devicePixelRatio||1,2);cv.width=cv.clientWidth*DPR;cv.height=cv.clientHeight*DPR;}
addEventListener("resize",()=>{resize();draw();});

function rot(p){
  let x=p[0]-center[0],y=p[1]-center[1],z=p[2]-center[2];
  const cy=Math.cos(yaw),sy=Math.sin(yaw); let x1=x*cy+z*sy,z1=-x*sy+z*cy;
  const cp=Math.cos(pitch),sp=Math.sin(pitch); let y2=y*cp-z1*sp,z2=y*sp+z1*cp;
  return [x1,y2,z2];
}
function proj(r){ const depth=r[2]+ext*dist; if(depth<=1)return null;
  const f=Math.min(cv.width,cv.height)*0.9; return [cv.width/2+r[0]*f/depth, cv.height/2-r[1]*f/depth, depth]; }

// affine texture-mapped triangle: map texture-space (uv in px) -> screen triangle
function texTri(img, sx0,sy0,sx1,sy1,sx2,sy2, u0,v0,u1,v1,u2,v2){
  const d = u0*(v1-v2)-v0*(u1-u2)+(u1*v2-u2*v1);
  if(Math.abs(d)<1e-6) return;
  const a=(sx0*(v1-v2)-v0*(sx1-sx2)+(sx1*v2-sx2*v1))/d;
  const b=(u0*(sx1-sx2)-sx0*(u1-u2)+(u1*sx2-u2*sx1))/d;
  const c=(u0*(v1*sx2-v2*sx1)-v0*(u1*sx2-u2*sx1)+sx0*(u1*v2-u2*v1))/d;
  const e=(sy0*(v1-v2)-v0*(sy1-sy2)+(sy1*v2-sy2*v1))/d;
  const f=(u0*(sy1-sy2)-sy0*(u1-u2)+(u1*sy2-u2*sy1))/d;
  const g=(u0*(v1*sy2-v2*sy1)-v0*(u1*sy2-u2*sy1)+sy0*(u1*v2-u2*v1))/d;
  ctx.save();
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(sx0,sy0); ctx.lineTo(sx1,sy1); ctx.lineTo(sx2,sy2); ctx.closePath(); ctx.clip();
  ctx.setTransform(a,e,b,f,c,g);
  ctx.drawImage(img,0,0);
  ctx.restore();
  ctx.setTransform(1,0,0,1,0,0);
}

function draw(){
  const css=getComputedStyle(document.documentElement);
  ctx.setTransform(1,0,0,1,0,0);
  ctx.fillStyle=css.getPropertyValue("--bg"); ctx.fillRect(0,0,cv.width,cv.height);
  if(!M) return;
  const P=M.pos.map(rot), S=P.map(proj);
  const L=[0.4,0.6,-0.68];
  const faces=[];
  for(let ti=0;ti<M.tris.length;ti++){
    const t=M.tris[ti]; const a=S[t[0]],b=S[t[1]],c=S[t[2]]; if(!a||!b||!c) continue;
    const z=(a[2]+b[2]+c[2])/3;
    const ra=P[t[0]],rb=P[t[1]],rc=P[t[2]];
    const u=[rb[0]-ra[0],rb[1]-ra[1],rb[2]-ra[2]], v=[rc[0]-ra[0],rc[1]-ra[1],rc[2]-ra[2]];
    let nx=u[1]*v[2]-u[2]*v[1], ny=u[2]*v[0]-u[0]*v[2], nz=u[0]*v[1]-u[1]*v[0];
    const ln=Math.hypot(nx,ny,nz)||1; let sh=Math.abs((nx*L[0]+ny*L[1]+nz*L[2])/ln);
    faces.push([z,ti,sh]);
  }
  faces.sort((p,q)=>q[0]-p[0]);
  for(const [z,ti,sh] of faces){
    const t=M.tris[ti]; const a=S[t[0]],b=S[t[1]],c=S[t[2]];
    const tex = useTex ? M.triTex[ti] : null;
    if(tex && M.uv.length){
      const tw=tex.width, th=tex.height;
      const ua=M.uv[t[0]],ub=M.uv[t[1]],uc=M.uv[t[2]];
      texTri(tex, a[0],a[1],b[0],b[1],c[0],c[1],
        ua[0]*tw,ua[1]*th, ub[0]*tw,ub[1]*th, uc[0]*tw,uc[1]*th);
    } else {
      const g=Math.round(40+sh*175);
      ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(a[0],a[1]);ctx.lineTo(b[0],b[1]);ctx.lineTo(c[0],c[1]);ctx.closePath();
      ctx.fillStyle=`rgb(${(g*0.55)|0},${(g*0.78)|0},${g})`; ctx.fill();
    }
  }
}
function loop(){ if(spin){yaw+=0.006; draw();} raf=requestAnimationFrame(loop); }

function fit(){
  const mn=[1e9,1e9,1e9],mx=[-1e9,-1e9,-1e9];
  for(const p of M.pos)for(let k=0;k<3;k++){mn[k]=Math.min(mn[k],p[k]);mx[k]=Math.max(mx[k],p[k]);}
  center=[(mn[0]+mx[0])/2,(mn[1]+mx[1])/2,(mn[2]+mx[2])/2];
  ext=Math.max(1,Math.max(mx[0]-mn[0],mx[1]-mn[1],mx[2]-mn[2]));
}
const texCanvasCache=new Map();
function texToCanvas(r){ const c=document.createElement("canvas"); c.width=r.w; c.height=r.h;
  const x=c.getContext("2d"); const im=x.createImageData(r.w,r.h); im.data.set(r.rgba); x.putImageData(im,0,0);
  return c; }   // NOTE: never re-assign c.width after drawing — it clears the canvas
// decode a texture by exact name (cached) -> canvas or null
function texByName(name){
  const up=name.toUpperCase();
  if(texCanvasCache.has(up)) return texCanvasCache.get(up);
  let out=null;
  for(const im of IMG){
    const e=im.toc.find(t=>t.name.toUpperCase()===up);
    if(e){ try{ out=texToCanvas(window.decodePTX(im.ab.slice(e.off,e.end), EXE)); }catch(_){ out=null; } break; }
  }
  texCanvasCache.set(up,out); return out;
}
// resolve a group's texture. The shape's own skin SHAPE\<name> is the verified-correct
// texture (e.g. ALIEN\GIRL -> SHAPE\ALIEN\GIRL). A numeric group index "NN" is a per-MAP
// material slot filled at map-load time from the map's own STAGE\<map>\* textures (NOT the
// global TEXTURE\NN, which are generic effects atlases) — so it can't be resolved statically
// here; leave those groups untextured rather than show the wrong skin. (SPEC §2.9.)
function groupTex(shapeName, tex4){
  return texByName("SHAPE\\"+shapeName);
}

function load(idx){
  const e=SHAPES[idx];
  const r=window.AXISShape.parseShape(SHAPE, e.off, e.end);
  M={pos:r.pos,uv:r.uv,tris:r.tris,name:e.name,groups:r.groups};
  // per-triangle texture: resolve each group's skin
  const triTex=new Array(r.tris.length).fill(null);
  let first=null, textured=0;
  for(const g of r.groups){
    const tx=groupTex(e.name, g.tex4);
    if(tx){ for(let ti=g.triStart;ti<g.triEnd;ti++){ triTex[ti]=tx; textured++; } if(!first)first=tx; }
  }
  M.triTex=triTex; TEX=first;
  const tv=document.getElementById("texview");
  if(first){ const txc=tv.getContext("2d"); txc.imageSmoothingEnabled=false; txc.clearRect(0,0,96,96); txc.drawImage(first,0,0,96,96); tv.style.display="block"; }
  else tv.style.display="none";
  fit();
  const nTex=r.groups.filter(g=>groupTex(e.name,g.tex4)).length;
  document.getElementById("stats").innerHTML=
    `<span>shape</span><b>${e.name}</b><span>verts</span><b>${r.pos.length}</b><span>tris</span><b>${r.tris.length}</b>`+
    `<span>groups</span><b>${r.groups.length}</b><span>textured</span><b>${nTex}/${r.groups.length} grp</b>`;
  hud.textContent=first?`textured · ${textured}/${r.tris.length} tris`:"no matching skin";
  draw(); try{localStorage.setItem("axtex",idx);}catch(_){}
}

let drag=null;
cv.addEventListener("pointerdown",e=>{drag={x:e.clientX,y:e.clientY};cv.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);});
cv.addEventListener("pointermove",e=>{ if(!drag)return; yaw-=(e.clientX-drag.x)*0.01;
  pitch=Math.max(-1.4,Math.min(1.4,pitch+(e.clientY-drag.y)*0.01)); drag.x=e.clientX;drag.y=e.clientY; if(!spin)draw(); });
cv.addEventListener("pointerup",()=>drag=null);
cv.addEventListener("wheel",e=>{e.preventDefault();dist=Math.max(0.6,Math.min(8,dist*(e.deltaY>0?1.1:0.9)));if(!spin)draw();},{passive:false});
document.getElementById("tex").addEventListener("change",e=>{useTex=e.target.checked;draw();});
document.getElementById("spin").addEventListener("change",e=>{spin=e.target.checked;});

async function init(){
  try{
    hud.textContent="loading game files…";
    EXE=await (await fetch("/game/AXIS.EXE")).arrayBuffer();
    SHAPE=await (await fetch("/game/Data/Shape.dat")).arrayBuffer();
    const toc=window.AXISShape.readTOC(SHAPE);
    // load all Image*.dat TOCs
    for(let i=0;i<=5;i++){
      try{ const ab=await (await fetch(`/game/Data/Image${i}.dat`)).arrayBuffer();
        IMG.push({ab, toc:window.AXISShape.readTOC(ab)}); }catch(e){}
    }
    const texNames=new Set();
    for(const im of IMG) for(const e of im.toc) texNames.add(e.name.toUpperCase());
    // keep meshes whose own skin SHAPE\<name> exists (the verified-correct textures)
    SHAPES=toc.filter(e=>texNames.has(("SHAPE\\"+e.name).toUpperCase()));
    const sel=document.getElementById("sel");
    sel.innerHTML=SHAPES.map((e,i)=>`<option value="${i}">${e.name}</option>`).join("");
    document.querySelector(".sub").textContent=`${SHAPES.length} meshes with their real skin · live from Shape.dat + Image*.dat`;
    let start=SHAPES.findIndex(e=>e.name.toUpperCase().endsWith("GIRL")); if(start<0)start=0;
    try{const s=+localStorage.getItem("axtex"); if(s>=0&&s<SHAPES.length)start=s;}catch(_){}
    sel.value=start; sel.addEventListener("change",()=>load(+sel.value));
    resize(); load(start); loop();
  }catch(err){ hud.textContent="Run `python run.py` first (needs /game/). "+err.message; }
}
init();
</script>
</body>
</html>
```

`web/shp.js`:

```js
/* AXIS Shape.dat archive + .shp mesh reader, in the browser engine.
 *
 * JS port of tools/parse_archive.py + tools/parse_shp.py + tools/export_shapes.py (merge).
 * Lets the client load 3D meshes straight from the user's own Shape.dat in the browser —
 * the asset loading the WASM client (approach B) ultimately does. See docs/SPEC.md §2.1/§2.8.
 *
 *   readTOC(datArrayBuffer)                 -> [{name, off, end}]
 *   parseShape(datArrayBuffer, off, end)    -> {pos:[[x,y,z]], nrm, uv, tris:[[a,b,c]],
 *                                               groups:[{tex4, triStart, triEnd}]}  (groups merged)
 *   tex4 is the group's stored texture index ("01".."NN", "" if none): the per-group skin
 *   resolves to the "TEXTURE\<tex4>" PTX entry (SPEC §2.9).
 */
(function (root) {
  'use strict';
  const dec = (typeof TextDecoder !== 'undefined') ? new TextDecoder('euc-kr') : null;
  const REC = 0x44, NAME = 0x40, OBJ_HDR = 0x4c, GRP_HDR = 0x80, TAG = 0x71110300;

  function name64(u8, o) {
    let n = 0; while (n < NAME && u8[o + n]) n++;
    if (dec) return dec.decode(u8.subarray(o, o + n));
    let s = ''; for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) s += String.fromCharCode(u8[o + i]); return s;
  }

  function readTOC(ab) {
    const dv = new DataView(ab), u8 = new Uint8Array(ab), count = dv.getUint32(0, true), out = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
      const b = 4 + i * REC;
      out.push({ name: name64(u8, b), off: dv.getUint32(b + NAME, true) });
    }
    for (let i = 0; i < out.length; i++) out[i].end = (i + 1 < out.length) ? out[i + 1].off : ab.byteLength;
    return out;
  }

  function parseShape(ab, off, end) {
    const dv = new DataView(ab);
    const tag = dv.getUint32(off + 0x30, true);
    if (tag !== TAG) throw new Error('tag ' + tag.toString(16));
    const gcount = dv.getInt32(off + 0x10, true);
    const u8 = new Uint8Array(ab);
    const meta = [], tex4 = [];
    for (let g = 0; g < gcount; g++) {
      const gh = off + OBJ_HDR + g * GRP_HDR;
      meta.push(dv.getInt32(gh + 0x34, true));   // vertexCount
      tex4.push(name64(u8, gh + 4));             // stored per-group texture index ("01"..)
    }
    let p = off + OBJ_HDR + gcount * GRP_HDR;
    const pos = [], nrm = [], uv = [], tris = [], groups = [];
    for (let g = 0; g < gcount; g++) {
      const vc = meta[g], base = pos.length, triStart = tris.length;
      for (let i = 0; i < vc; i++) pos.push([dv.getFloat32(p + i * 12, true), dv.getFloat32(p + i * 12 + 4, true), dv.getFloat32(p + i * 12 + 8, true)]);
      p += vc * 12;
      for (let i = 0; i < vc; i++) nrm.push([dv.getFloat32(p + i * 12, true), dv.getFloat32(p + i * 12 + 4, true), dv.getFloat32(p + i * 12 + 8, true)]);
      p += vc * 12;
      p += vc * 4;                                // attr
      for (let i = 0; i < vc; i++) uv.push([dv.getFloat32(p + i * 8, true), dv.getFloat32(p + i * 8 + 4, true)]);
      p += vc * 8;
      // index region: triangles (3xu32 all < vc) interleaved with 12-byte descriptors
      // whose 3rd word is a stale saved pointer (0x04000000..0x40000000).
      while (p + 12 <= end) {
        const a = dv.getUint32(p, true), b = dv.getUint32(p + 4, true), c = dv.getUint32(p + 8, true);
        if (a < vc && b < vc && c < vc) { tris.push([a + base, b + base, c + base]); p += 12; }
        else if (c > 0x04000000 && c < 0x40000000) { p += 12; }
        else break;
      }
      groups.push({ tex4: tex4[g], triStart, triEnd: tris.length });
    }
    return { pos, nrm, uv, tris, groups, consumed: p - off, size: end - off };
  }

  root.AXISShape = { readTOC, parseShape };
  if (typeof module !== 'undefined' && module.exports) module.exports = { readTOC, parseShape };
})(typeof window !== 'undefined' ? window : globalThis);

```

`web/skel.js`:

```js
/* AXIS Skeleton.dat reader (bind-pose skeletons), in the browser engine.
 *
 * JS port of tools/parse_skeleton.py (see docs/SPEC.md §2.10). Reads the
 * plaintext indexed archive TOC, parses a skeleton's bone records, and builds
 * bind-pose world joint positions. Lets the client load a skeleton straight
 * from the user's own Data/Skeleton.dat in the browser.
 *
 *   listSkeletons(ab) -> [{name, offset, size}]
 *   parseSkeleton(ab, entry) -> {name, boneCount, bones:[{name,parent,t,angle,axis}]}
 *   worldJoints(sk) -> [[x,y,z], ...]   bind-pose joint positions, bone order
 */
(function (root) {
  'use strict';
  const dec = (typeof TextDecoder !== 'undefined') ? new TextDecoder('euc-kr') : null;
  const REC = 0x44, NAME = 0x40, STRIDE = 0x70, FIRST = 0x24;

  function cstr(u8, o, max) {
    let n = 0; while (n < max && u8[o + n]) n++;
    if (dec) return dec.decode(u8.subarray(o, o + n));
    let s = ''; for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) s += String.fromCharCode(u8[o + i]); return s;
  }

  function listSkeletons(ab) {
    const u8 = new Uint8Array(ab), dv = new DataView(ab);
    const count = dv.getUint32(0, true);
    const ent = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
      const base = 4 + i * REC;
      ent.push({ name: cstr(u8, base, NAME), offset: dv.getUint32(base + NAME, true) });
    }
    const out = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < ent.length; i++) {
      const end = i + 1 < ent.length ? ent[i + 1].offset : u8.length;
      out.push({ name: ent[i].name, offset: ent[i].offset, size: end - ent[i].offset });
    }
    return out;
  }

  function parseSkeleton(ab, entry) {
    const u8 = new Uint8Array(ab, entry.offset, entry.size);
    const dv = new DataView(u8.buffer, u8.byteOffset, u8.byteLength);
    const boneCount = dv.getInt32(0x10, true);
    const bones = [];
    for (let b = 0; b < boneCount; b++) {
      const bo = FIRST + b * STRIDE;
      if (bo + STRIDE > u8.length) break;
      bones.push({
        name: cstr(u8, bo, NAME),
        parent: dv.getInt32(bo + 0x40, true),
        t: [dv.getFloat32(bo + 0x44, true), dv.getFloat32(bo + 0x48, true), dv.getFloat32(bo + 0x4c, true)],
        angle: dv.getFloat32(bo + 0x50, true),
        axis: [dv.getFloat32(bo + 0x54, true), dv.getFloat32(bo + 0x58, true), dv.getFloat32(bo + 0x5c, true)]
      });
    }
    return { name: entry.name, boneCount, bones };
  }

  function axisAngle(th, ax, ay, az) {
    let n = Math.hypot(ax, ay, az) || 1; ax /= n; ay /= n; az /= n;
    const c = Math.cos(th), s = Math.sin(th), t = 1 - c;
    return [t*ax*ax+c, t*ax*ay-s*az, t*ax*az+s*ay, 0,
            t*ax*ay+s*az, t*ay*ay+c, t*ay*az-s*ax, 0,
            t*ax*az-s*ay, t*ay*az+s*ax, t*az*az+c, 0,
            0, 0, 0, 1];
  }
  function mul(a, b) {
    const r = new Array(16);
    for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) for (let j = 0; j < 4; j++)
      r[i*4+j] = a[i*4]*b[j] + a[i*4+1]*b[4+j] + a[i*4+2]*b[8+j] + a[i*4+3]*b[12+j];
    return r;
  }

  function worldJoints(sk) {
    const n = sk.bones.length, world = new Array(n).fill(null);
    function comp(i) {
      if (world[i]) return world[i];
      const b = sk.bones[i];
      const tl = [1,0,0,b.t[0], 0,1,0,b.t[1], 0,0,1,b.t[2], 0,0,0,1];
      const local = mul(tl, axisAngle(b.angle, b.axis[0], b.axis[1], b.axis[2]));
      world[i] = (b.parent < 0 || b.parent >= n) ? local : mul(comp(b.parent), local);
      return world[i];
    }
    const out = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) { const m = comp(i); out.push([m[3], m[7], m[11]]); }
    return out;
  }

  root.AXISSkel = { listSkeletons, parseSkeleton, worldJoints };
  if (typeof module !== 'undefined' && module.exports)
    module.exports = { listSkeletons, parseSkeleton, worldJoints };
})(typeof window !== 'undefined' ? window : globalThis);

```

```html
<script src="skel.js"></script>
<script>
const cv=document.getElementById("c"),ctx=cv.getContext("2d"),hud=document.getElementById("hud");
let DPR=Math.min(devicePixelRatio||1,2);
let SK=null, W=null, center=[0,0,0], ext=1, yaw=0.6, pitch=0.3, dist=2.3, spin=true, labels=false, raf=0;

function resize(){DPR=Math.min(devicePixelRatio||1,2);cv.width=cv.clientWidth*DPR;cv.height=cv.clientHeight*DPR;}
addEventListener("resize",()=>{resize();draw();});

function rot(p){
  let x=p[0]-center[0],y=p[1]-center[1],z=p[2]-center[2];
  const cy=Math.cos(yaw),sy=Math.sin(yaw); let x1=x*cy+z*sy,z1=-x*sy+z*cy;
  const cp=Math.cos(pitch),sp=Math.sin(pitch); let y2=y*cp-z1*sp,z2=y*sp+z1*cp;
  return [x1,y2,z2];
}
// AXIS rigs are authored with -Y "down the limb"; flip Y so they stand upright.
function proj(r){ const depth=r[2]+ext*dist; if(depth<=1)return null;
  const f=Math.min(cv.width,cv.height)*0.9; return [cv.width/2+r[0]*f/depth, cv.height/2-(-r[1])*f/depth, depth]; }

function draw(){
  const css=getComputedStyle(document.documentElement);
  ctx.fillStyle=css.getPropertyValue("--bg"); ctx.fillRect(0,0,cv.width,cv.height);
  if(!SK||!W) return;
  const P=W.map(rot), S=P.map(proj);
  const acc=css.getPropertyValue("--accent").trim(), rootc=css.getPropertyValue("--root").trim(),
        linec=css.getPropertyValue("--line").trim(), fg=css.getPropertyValue("--fg").trim();
  // bones (parent -> child), depth sorted far-to-near
  const segs=[];
  SK.bones.forEach((b,i)=>{ if(b.parent<0||b.parent>=S.length)return;
    const a=S[b.parent],c=S[i]; if(!a||!c)return;
    segs.push([(a[2]+c[2])/2,a,c]); });
  segs.sort((p,q)=>q[0]-p[0]);
  ctx.lineCap="round";
  for(const [z,a,c] of segs){
    const t=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,1-(z-ext*(dist-0.8))/(ext*1.6)));
    ctx.strokeStyle=acc; ctx.globalAlpha=0.35+0.55*t; ctx.lineWidth=(1.5+2.5*t)*DPR;
    ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(a[0],a[1]);ctx.lineTo(c[0],c[1]);ctx.stroke();
  }
  ctx.globalAlpha=1;
  // joints
  SK.bones.forEach((b,i)=>{ const s=S[i]; if(!s)return;
    const isRoot=b.parent<0||b.parent>=S.length;
    ctx.fillStyle=isRoot?rootc:acc;
    ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(s[0],s[1],(isRoot?4.5:3)*DPR,0,7);ctx.fill();
    if(labels){ ctx.fillStyle=fg; ctx.font=`${11*DPR}px var(--mono),monospace`;
      ctx.fillText(b.name,s[0]+6*DPR,s[1]-5*DPR); }
  });
}
function loop(){ if(spin){yaw+=0.006; draw();} raf=requestAnimationFrame(loop); }

function fit(){
  const mn=[1e9,1e9,1e9],mx=[-1e9,-1e9,-1e9];
  for(const p of W)for(let k=0;k<3;k++){mn[k]=Math.min(mn[k],p[k]);mx[k]=Math.max(mx[k],p[k]);}
  center=[(mn[0]+mx[0])/2,(mn[1]+mx[1])/2,(mn[2]+mx[2])/2];
  ext=Math.max(1,Math.max(mx[0]-mn[0],mx[1]-mn[1],mx[2]-mn[2]));
  const roots=SK.bones.filter(b=>b.parent<0).length;
  hud.textContent=`${SK.boneCount} bones · ${roots} root${roots===1?"":"s"} · size ${(mx[0]-mn[0])|0}×${(mx[1]-mn[1])|0}×${(mx[2]-mn[2])|0}`;
}
function show(name,bones,boneCount,world){
  SK={name,bones,boneCount}; W=world; fit();
  const roots=bones.filter(b=>b.parent<0).length;
  document.getElementById("stats").innerHTML=
    `<span>rig</span><b>${name}</b><span>bones</span><b>${boneCount}</b><span>roots</span><b>${roots}</b>`;
  draw();
}

let drag=null;
cv.addEventListener("pointerdown",e=>{drag={x:e.clientX,y:e.clientY};cv.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);});
cv.addEventListener("pointermove",e=>{ if(!drag)return; yaw-=(e.clientX-drag.x)*0.01;
  pitch=Math.max(-1.4,Math.min(1.4,pitch+(e.clientY-drag.y)*0.01)); drag.x=e.clientX;drag.y=e.clientY; if(!spin)draw(); });
cv.addEventListener("pointerup",()=>drag=null);
cv.addEventListener("wheel",e=>{e.preventDefault();dist=Math.max(0.6,Math.min(8,dist*(e.deltaY>0?1.1:0.9)));if(!spin)draw();},{passive:false});
document.getElementById("labels").addEventListener("change",e=>{labels=e.target.checked;draw();});
document.getElementById("spin").addEventListener("change",e=>{spin=e.target.checked;});

// Live mode: parse rigs in-browser straight from Skeleton.dat (skel.js), no pre-export.
async function initLive(){
  const ab=await (await fetch("/game/Data/Skeleton.dat")).arrayBuffer();
  const toc=window.AXISSkel.listSkeletons(ab);
  const sel=document.getElementById("sel");
  sel.innerHTML=toc.map((e,i)=>`<option value="${i}">${e.name}</option>`).join("");
  function loadIdx(i){ const e=toc[i];
    try{ const sk=window.AXISSkel.parseSkeleton(ab,e);
      if(!sk.bones.length){hud.textContent="(no bones)";return;}
      show(e.name,sk.bones,sk.boneCount,window.AXISSkel.worldJoints(sk));
      try{localStorage.setItem("axskeli",i);}catch(_){}
    }catch(err){ hud.textContent="decode: "+err.message; }
  }
  let start=toc.findIndex(e=>e.name.toUpperCase().includes("BODY")); if(start<0)start=0;
  try{const s=+localStorage.getItem("axskeli"); if(s>=0&&s<toc.length)start=s;}catch(e){}
  sel.value=start; sel.addEventListener("change",()=>loadIdx(+sel.value));
  document.querySelector(".sub").textContent=`live from Skeleton.dat · ${toc.length} rigs (skel.js)`;
  resize(); loadIdx(start); loop();
}
// fall back to pre-exported JSON (python run.py) if /game/ isn't served
function initJSON(){
  fetch("skeletons/index.json").then(r=>r.json()).then(list=>{
    const sel=document.getElementById("sel");
    sel.innerHTML=list.map((e,i)=>`<option value="${i}">${e.name}  (${e.bones} bones)</option>`).join("");
    function loadIdx(i){ fetch("skeletons/"+list[i].file).then(r=>r.json()).then(d=>{
      show(d.name,d.bones,d.boneCount,d.world||window.AXISSkel.worldJoints(d));
      try{localStorage.setItem("axskeli",i);}catch(_){}
    });}
    let start=0; try{const s=+localStorage.getItem("axskeli"); if(s>=0&&s<list.length)start=s;}catch(e){}
    sel.value=start; sel.addEventListener("change",()=>loadIdx(+sel.value));
    document.querySelector(".sub").textContent=`bind-pose rigs from Skeleton.dat · ${list.length} rigs`;
    resize(); loadIdx(start); loop();
  }).catch(()=>{ resize(); hud.textContent="Run `python run.py` first."; });
}
fetch("/game/Data/Skeleton.dat",{method:"HEAD"}).then(r=>{ if(r.ok&&window.AXISSkel) initLive(); else initJSON(); }).catch(initJSON);
</script>
</body>
</html>

```

```html
  <h1>AXIS <span>Texture Gallery</span></h1>
  <input id="filter" type="text" placeholder="filter… (e.g. ARENA, METAL, ALPHA)" autocomplete="off">
  <span class="count" id="count"></span>
  <a href="index.html">← explorer</a>

<script>
let ALL=[];
const grid=document.getElementById("grid"), fin=document.getElementById("filter"), cnt=document.getElementById("count");
function render(q){
  q=q.trim().toLowerCase();
  const items=q?ALL.filter(t=>t.name.toLowerCase().includes(q)):ALL;
  cnt.textContent=items.length+" / "+ALL.length;
  if(!items.length){grid.innerHTML="<div class='empty'>No textures match.</div>";return;}
  grid.innerHTML="";
  const frag=document.createDocumentFragment();
  for(const t of items.slice(0,600)){
    const c=document.createElement("div"); c.className="cell";
    c.innerHTML=`<div class="imgwrap"><img loading="lazy" src="textures/${t.id}.png" alt="${t.name}"></div>`+
      `<div class="cap"><b>${t.name}</b><br>${t.w}×${t.h} · ${t.src}</div>`;
    frag.appendChild(c);
  }
  grid.appendChild(frag);
  if(items.length>600){ const m=document.createElement("div"); m.className="empty";
    m.textContent=`showing first 600 of ${items.length} — filter to narrow`; grid.appendChild(m); }
}
fin.addEventListener("input",e=>render(e.target.value));
fetch("textures/index.json").then(r=>r.json()).then(d=>{ ALL=d; render(""); })
  .catch(()=>{ grid.innerHTML="<div class='empty'>Run <code>python run.py</code> to decode textures.</div>"; });
</script>
</body>
</html>
```

`web/wasm.html`:

```html
<!doctype html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
<meta charset="utf-8">
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1">
<title>AXIS WASM Decrypt</title>
<style>
  :root{ --bg:#0e1420; --panel:#151d2c; --panel2:#1b2638; --line:#263349;
    --fg:#e6edf6; --muted:#8aa0bd; --accent:#38d0c8; --ok:#6fe3a0; --bad:#f57b7b;
    --mono:ui-monospace,"Cascadia Code",Menlo,Consolas,monospace;
    --sans:ui-sans-serif,system-ui,"Segoe UI",Roboto,sans-serif; color-scheme:dark; }
  @media (prefers-color-scheme:light){ :root{ --bg:#eef2f7; --panel:#fff; --panel2:#f4f7fb;
    --line:#d6deea; --fg:#172031; --muted:#5b6b85; --accent:#0e8f88; --ok:#1c9c5f; --bad:#c23b3b; color-scheme:light; } }
  *{box-sizing:border-box}
  body{margin:0;background:var(--bg);color:var(--fg);font:14px/1.6 var(--sans);
    padding:28px max(16px,env(safe-area-inset-right)) 60px max(16px,env(safe-area-inset-left))}
  .wrap{max-width:860px;margin:0 auto}
  h1{font:600 22px/1.2 var(--sans);margin:0} h1 span{color:var(--accent)}
  .sub{color:var(--muted);margin:4px 0 20px}
  a{color:var(--accent)}
  .card{background:var(--panel);border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:12px;padding:18px 20px;margin-bottom:18px}
  .badge{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;font:600 13px/1 var(--mono);
    background:var(--panel2);border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:999px;padding:8px 14px}
  .dot{width:9px;height:9px;border-radius:50%;background:var(--accent)}
  table{width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;font:12.5px/1.5 var(--mono)}
  th,td{text-align:left;padding:7px 10px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--line)}
  th{color:var(--muted);font-weight:600;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.08em;font-size:11px}
  .ok{color:var(--ok);font-weight:700} .bad{color:var(--bad);font-weight:700}
  .big{font:700 15px/1 var(--mono)}
  .hint{color:var(--muted);font-size:12px;margin-top:10px}
</style>
</head>
<body>
<div class="wrap">
  <h1>AXIS <span>WASM Decrypt</span></h1>
  <div class="sub">the reconstructed obfuscation cipher, compiled to WebAssembly, running in your browser</div>

  <div class="card">
    <div id="backend" class="badge"><span class="dot"></span>loading…</div>
    <div class="hint">Module: <code>port/src/axis_crypt.c</code> → WebAssembly (<code>web/crypt.js</code>,
      embedded). Each file below is decrypted by the WASM module and checked, byte-for-byte,
      against the reference cipher — on your own game files, served at <code>/game/</code>.</div>
  </div>

  <div class="card">
    <table id="tbl"><thead><tr><th>file</th><th>bytes</th><th>skip</th><th>version</th><th>WASM = reference</th></tr></thead>
      <tbody id="rows"><tr><td colspan="5" class="hint">decrypting…</td></tr></tbody></table>
    <div id="summary" class="big" style="margin-top:14px"></div>
  </div>

  <div class="hint"><a href="home.html">← home</a> · This is the approach-B path: real reconstructed
    AXIS logic as WASM. The same module decrypts Map/*.bfd, *.ape and Sound.dat for the other viewers.</div>
</div>
<script src="crypt.js"></script>
<script>
const TARGETS = [
  ["Map/CUBE.bfd", 4], ["Map/CrimsonField.bfd", 4],
  ["AutoPlay/Autoplay001.ape", 0], ["Data/Sound.dat", 0],
];
function hex4(u8){ return "0x" + [...u8.slice(0,4)].reverse().map(b=>b.toString(16).padStart(2,"0")).join("").replace(/^0+(?=.)/,""); }

(async function(){
  const C = await window.AXISCrypt.load();
  const be = document.getElementById("backend");
  be.innerHTML = `<span class="dot"></span>backend: ${C.backend.toUpperCase()}` +
    (C.backend==="wasm" ? "  ·  WebAssembly instantiated ✓" : "  ·  JS fallback (WASM unavailable)");
  const rows = document.getElementById("rows"); rows.innerHTML = "";
  let pass=0, fail=0, any=false;
  for (const [rel, skip] of TARGETS){
    let r;
    try { r = await fetch("/game/"+rel); if(!r.ok) continue; } catch(e){ continue; }
    any=true;
    const raw = new Uint8Array(await r.arrayBuffer());
    const a = raw.slice(), b = raw.slice();
    C.decrypt(a, skip);                       // WASM (or fallback)
    window.AXISCrypt.jsDecrypt(b, skip);      // pure-JS reference
    let same = a.length===b.length;
    for (let i=0;same&&i<a.length;i++) if(a[i]!==b[i]) same=false;
    same ? pass++ : fail++;
    const tr = document.createElement("tr");
    tr.innerHTML = `<td>${rel}</td><td>${raw.length.toLocaleString()}</td><td>${skip}</td>`+
      `<td>${hex4(a)}</td><td class="${same?'ok':'bad'}">${same?'PASS':'FAIL'}</td>`;
    rows.appendChild(tr);
  }
  if(!any){ rows.innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="5" class="hint">No game files at /game/ — run <code>python run.py</code>.</td></tr>`; return; }
  const s = document.getElementById("summary");
  s.innerHTML = fail ? `<span class="bad">${fail} FAILED</span>, ${pass} passed`
    : `<span class="ok">ALL ${pass} PASS</span> — WASM decrypt is byte-identical to the reference on your files`;
})();
</script>
</body>
</html>

```
